    var cVerified = 0, cUnverified = 0, cNoResp = 0, cUnreach = 0;
    
    pData.forEach(function(r) {
      var st = String(r.status_post_disb || window.getCellValue(r, 'status_post_disb') || '').trim();
      if (st === 'Sudah Post Disb') cVerified++;
      else if (st === 'Tidak Direspon') cNoResp++;
      else if (st === 'Tidak Bisa Dihubungi') cUnreach++;
      else cUnverified++;
    });

    var totPD = pData.length;
    var pctVer = totPD > 0 ? ((cVerified / totPD) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';
    var pctUnv = totPD > 0 ? ((cUnverified / totPD) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';
    var pctNoR = totPD > 0 ? ((cNoResp / totPD) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';
    var pctUnr = totPD > 0 ? ((cUnreach / totPD) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';

    var elVerPct = document.getElementById('pdMetricVerifiedPct');
    var elVerCnt = document.getElementById('pdMetricVerifiedCount');
    var elUnvPct = document.getElementById('pdMetricUnverifiedPct');
    var elUnvCnt = document.getElementById('pdMetricUnverifiedCount');
    var elNoRPct = document.getElementById('pdMetricNoResponsePct');
    var elNoRCnt = document.getElementById('pdMetricNoResponseCount');
    var elUnrPct = document.getElementById('pdMetricUnreachablePct');
    var elUnrCnt = document.getElementById('pdMetricUnreachableCount');

    if (elVerPct) elVerPct.textContent = pctVer;
    if (elVerCnt) elVerCnt.textContent = cVerified + ' Mitra Terverifikasi';
    if (elUnvPct) elUnvPct.textContent = pctUnv;
    if (elUnvCnt) elUnvCnt.textContent = cUnverified + ' Mitra Belum Post Disb';
    if (elNoRPct) elNoRPct.textContent = pctNoR;
    if (elNoRCnt) elNoRCnt.textContent = cNoResp + ' Mitra Tidak Merespon';
    if (elUnrPct) elUnrPct.textContent = pctUnr;
    if (elUnrCnt) elUnrCnt.textContent = cUnreach + ' Mitra Nomor Tidak Aktif';

    var pdContainer = document.getElementById('postDisbMetricsContainer');
    if (pdContainer) pdContainer.style.display = 'grid';
    return;
  }

  // 2. Highlight Card Monitoring GD
  if (key === 'monitoringGD') {
    var totAmountGD1 = 0, totAmountGD2 = 0;
    var mitrasGD1 = {}, mitrasGD2 = {};
    var newMitraGD1 = 0, lanMitraGD1 = 0;
    var newMitraGD2 = 0, lanMitraGD2 = 0;

    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var plf = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'plafond') || r['_raw_10']);
      var gdType = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'gd_2') || r['_raw_14'] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      var borrower = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'borrower') || window.getCellValue(r, 'namamitra') || r['_raw_2'] || '').trim();
      var katMitra = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'kategori_mitra') || r['_raw_18'] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var isNew = (katMitra.indexOf('baru') !== -1 || katMitra.indexOf('new') !== -1);
      var isLan = (katMitra.indexOf('lanjut') !== -1 || katMitra.indexOf('subsequent') !== -1);

      if (gdType.indexOf('GD 1') !== -1 || gdType === '1' || gdType === 'GD1') {
        totAmountGD1 += plf;
        if (borrower) mitrasGD1[borrower.toLowerCase()] = true;
        if (isNew) newMitraGD1++;
        else if (isLan) lanMitraGD1++;
      } else if (gdType.indexOf('GD 2') !== -1 || gdType === '2' || gdType === 'GD2') {
        totAmountGD2 += plf;
        if (borrower) mitrasGD2[borrower.toLowerCase()] = true;
        if (isNew) newMitraGD2++;
        else if (isLan) lanMitraGD2++;
      }
    });

    var countMitraGD1 = Object.keys(mitrasGD1).length;
    var countMitraGD2 = Object.keys(mitrasGD2).length;

    var m = window.monitoringGdMetrics || {};
    var kpSudahNom = m.kpSudahCairNominal || 0;
    var kpSudahCnt = m.kpSudahCairCount || 0;
    var kpMenungguNom = m.kpMenungguCairNominal || 0;
    var kpMenungguCnt = m.kpMenungguCairCount || 0;
    var totalKpGroupAmount = m.totalKpAmount || (kpSudahNom + kpMenungguNom);

    var pctGD1 = totalKpGroupAmount > 0 ? ((totAmountGD1 / totalKpGroupAmount) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';
    var pctGD2 = totalKpGroupAmount > 0 ? ((totAmountGD2 / totalKpGroupAmount) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';

    cards = [
      {
        label: 'Total Amount GD 1',
        val: 'Rp ' + totAmountGD1.toLocaleString('id-ID'),
        icon: 'GD1',
        note: countMitraGD1 + ' Mitra (' + newMitraGD1 + ' Baru / ' + lanMitraGD1 + ' Lanjutan) • Rasio: ' + pctGD1
      },
      {
        label: 'Total Amount GD 2',
        val: 'Rp ' + totAmountGD2.toLocaleString('id-ID'),
        icon: 'GD2',
        note: countMitraGD2 + ' Mitra (' + newMitraGD2 + ' Baru / ' + lanMitraGD2 + ' Lanjutan) • Rasio: ' + pctGD2
      },
      {
        label: 'Group Loan Sudah Cair (KP Daily)',
        val: 'Rp ' + kpSudahNom.toLocaleString('id-ID'),
        icon: '✓',
        note: kpSudahCnt + ' Mitra Terkonfirmasi Cair'
      },
      {
        label: 'Group Loan Menunggu Cair (KP Daily)',
        val: 'Rp ' + kpMenungguNom.toLocaleString('id-ID'),
        icon: '⏳',
        note: kpMenungguCnt + ' Mitra Siap Cair • Total KP: Rp ' + totalKpGroupAmount.toLocaleString('id-ID')
      }
    ];

  // 3. Highlight Dashboard (Tersinkronisasi Penuh dengan Data Riil Termasuk SOS Aman/Palsu & Zero-ID Guard)
  } else if (key === 'dashboard') {
    var uniqueBPs = [];
    var pointsSet = {};
    var uniqueEligibleMitras = {};
    var uniqueDOMitras = {};
    var uniqueSOSMitras = {};

    var eligibleMinat = 0, eligibleTdkMinat = 0;
    var doMinat = 0, doTdkMinat = 0;
    var sosAman = 0, sosPalsu = 0;

    var newParVisit = 0, newParCol = 0;
    var dpd130Visit = 0, dpd130Col = 0;
    var dpd3160Visit = 0, dpd3160Col = 0;
    var dpd6190Visit = 0, dpd6190Col = 0;
    var wobVisit = 0, wobCol = 0;

    var valMajelisSet = {};
    var valMitraVisit = 0, valMitraValid = 0;
    var supMajelisSet = {};
    var supMitraTotal = 0;

    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var rawBp = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'bpbm') || window.getCellValue(r, 'bp') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || r['_raw_1'] || '').trim();
      var pt = window.getCellValue(r, 'point') || window.getCellValue(r, 'branch') || r['_raw_2'] || '';
      
      if (rawBp && rawBp !== '-' && rawBp.toLowerCase() !== 'undefined') {
        var foundBp = false;
        for (var b = 0; b < uniqueBPs.length; b++) {
          if (window.calculateNameSimilarity(uniqueBPs[b], rawBp) >= 0.90) {
            foundBp = true;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (!foundBp) uniqueBPs.push(rawBp);
      }
      if (pt) pointsSet[pt] = true;

      // ZETTBOT ZERO-ID GUARD: Jika ID Loan adalah '0' atau kosong, WAJIB fallback ke nama mitra!
      var rawId = window.getCellValue(r, 'idloan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'id_loan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'loan_id') || '';
      var cleanId = window.normalizeNumericId(rawId);
      var namaMitra = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'namamitra') || window.getCellValue(r, 'namacalonmitra') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama_mitra') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var mitraKey = cleanId ? ('LOAN_' + cleanId) : (namaMitra ? ('NM_' + namaMitra) : ('ROW_' + Math.random()));

      var act = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'aktivitas') || r['_raw_4'] || '').toLowerCase();
      var ket = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'keterangan') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var hst = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'hasilvisit') || r['_raw_8'] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var colNom = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'nominalcollect') || r['_raw_10']);
      var majelisName = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'majelis') || r['_raw_5'] || r['_raw_4'] || '').trim().toLowerCase();

      var isMinat = (ket === 'minat' || (ket.indexOf('minat') !== -1 && ket.indexOf('tidak') === -1 && ket.indexOf('belum') === -1 && ket.indexOf('blm') === -1)) ||
                    (hst.indexOf('minat') !== -1 && hst.indexOf('tidak') === -1 && hst.indexOf('belum') === -1 && hst.indexOf('blm') === -1);

      if (act.indexOf('eligible') !== -1 || act.indexOf('wl') !== -1) {
        if (!uniqueEligibleMitras[mitraKey]) {
          uniqueEligibleMitras[mitraKey] = true;
          if (isMinat) eligibleMinat++; else eligibleTdkMinat++;
        }
      } else if (act.indexOf('do') !== -1 || act.indexOf('drop out') !== -1) {
        if (!uniqueDOMitras[mitraKey]) {
          uniqueDOMitras[mitraKey] = true;
          if (isMinat) doMinat++; else doTdkMinat++;
        }
      } else if (act.indexOf('sos') !== -1 || act.indexOf('sosialisasi') !== -1 || r.is_sosialisasi || r.namacalonmitra) {
        if (!uniqueSOSMitras[mitraKey]) {
          uniqueSOSMitras[mitraKey] = true;
          var kText = (ket + ' ' + hst).toLowerCase();
          if (kText.indexOf('palsu') !== -1 || kText.indexOf('potensi') !== -1) sosPalsu++; else sosAman++;
        }
      } else if (act.indexOf('new par') !== -1 || act.indexOf('newpar') !== -1) {
        newParVisit++; newParCol += colNom;
      } else if (act.indexOf('8-30') !== -1 || act.indexOf('830') !== -1 || act.indexOf('par 8-30') !== -1 || act.indexOf('dpd 8-30') !== -1 || act.indexOf('par 1-30') !== -1 || act.indexOf('dpd 1-30') !== -1) {
        dpd130Visit++; dpd130Col += colNom;
      } else if (act.indexOf('31-60') !== -1 || act.indexOf('3160') !== -1 || act.indexOf('par 31-60') !== -1 || act.indexOf('dpd 31-60') !== -1) {
        dpd3160Visit++; dpd3160Col += colNom;
      } else if (act.indexOf('61-90') !== -1 || act.indexOf('6190') !== -1 || act.indexOf('par 61-90') !== -1 || act.indexOf('dpd 61-90') !== -1) {
        dpd6190Visit++; dpd6190Col += colNom;
      } else if (act.indexOf('wob') !== -1) {
        wobVisit++; wobCol += colNom;
      } else if (act.indexOf('validasi') !== -1) {
        if (majelisName) valMajelisSet[majelisName] = true;
        valMitraVisit++;
        var validKws = ['done', 'vuk', 'sesuai', 'selesai', 'aprove', 'aproval', 'valid'];
        var isValid = validKws.some(function(kw) { return (ket + ' ' + hst).indexOf(kw) !== -1; });
        if (isValid) valMitraValid++;
      } else if (act.indexOf('suprise') !== -1 || act.indexOf('surprise') !== -1) {
        if (majelisName) supMajelisSet[majelisName] = true;
        supMitraTotal += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'jumlahmitra') || r['_raw_6']);
      }
    });

    // Sinkronisasi data sosialisasi langsung jika disediakan backend
    var totalSosCount = Object.keys(uniqueSOSMitras).length;
    if (totalSosCount === 0 && window.dashboardMetrics && window.dashboardMetrics.sosTotal) {
      totalSosCount = window.dashboardMetrics.sosTotal;
      sosAman = window.dashboardMetrics.sosAman;
      sosPalsu = window.dashboardMetrics.sosPotensiPalsu;
    }

    var countValMajelis = Object.keys(valMajelisSet).length;
    var pctValidasi = valMitraVisit > 0 ? ((valMitraValid / valMitraVisit) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';
    var countSupMajelis = Object.keys(supMajelisSet).length;
    var avgSupMitra = countSupMajelis > 0 ? (supMitraTotal / countSupMajelis).toFixed(1) : '0';

    cards = [
      { label: 'Total BP/BM', val: uniqueBPs.length, icon: 'BP', note: 'BP/BM unik (Fuzzy 90%)' },
      { label: 'Total Point', val: Object.keys(pointsSet).length, icon: 'PT', note: 'Point cabang terdeteksi' },
      { label: 'Kunjungan Eligible (WL)', val: Object.keys(uniqueEligibleMitras).length, icon: 'E', note: eligibleMinat + ' Minat / ' + eligibleTdkMinat + ' Tdk Minat' },
      { label: 'Kunjungan DO', val: Object.keys(uniqueDOMitras).length, icon: 'DO', note: doMinat + ' Minat / ' + doTdkMinat + ' Tdk Minat' },
      { label: 'Sosialisasi Calon Mitra', val: totalSosCount, icon: 'S', note: sosAman + ' Aman / ' + sosPalsu + ' Potensi Palsu' },
      { label: 'Validasi UK / Modal BM', val: valMitraVisit, icon: 'V', note: valMitraValid + ' Valid (' + pctValidasi + ') • ' + countValMajelis + ' Majelis' },
      { label: 'Kolek New Par', val: newParVisit + ' Visit', icon: 'NP', note: 'Rp ' + newParCol.toLocaleString('id-ID') },
      { label: 'Kolek DPD 8-30', val: dpd130Visit + ' Visit', icon: 'D1', note: 'Rp ' + dpd130Col.toLocaleString('id-ID') },
      { label: 'Kolek DPD 31-60', val: dpd3160Visit + ' Visit', icon: 'D2', note: 'Rp ' + dpd3160Col.toLocaleString('id-ID') },
      { label: 'Kolek DPD 61-90', val: dpd6190Visit + ' Visit', icon: 'D3', note: 'Rp ' + dpd6190Col.toLocaleString('id-ID') },
      { label: 'Kolek WOB', val: wobVisit + ' Visit', icon: 'WB', note: 'Rp ' + wobCol.toLocaleString('id-ID') },
      { label: 'Surprise Visit', val: countSupMajelis + ' Majelis', icon: 'SV', note: supMitraTotal + ' Mitra (Rata ' + avgSupMitra + '/Majelis)' }
    ];

  // 4. Highlight Card KPI RM / AM / BM
  } else if (key === 'kpiRM' || key === 'kpiAM' || key === 'kpiBM') {
    var totPlfNew = 0, sumScoreNew = 0, totPlfRen = 0, sumScoreRen = 0;
    var sumRep0 = 0, sumScore0 = 0, sumScore130 = 0, sumScore3190 = 0;
    var lenK = data.length || 1;

    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var idxNew = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 7 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 8 : 9;
      var idxScoreNew = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 9 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 10 : 11;
      var idxRen = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 11 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 12 : 13;
      var idxScoreRen = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 13 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 14 : 15;
      var idxRep0 = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 15 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 16 : 17;
      var idxScore0 = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 17 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 18 : 19;
      var idxScore130 = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 21 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 22 : 23;
      var idxScore3190 = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 25 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 26 : 27;

      totPlfNew += window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idxNew] || window.getCellValue(r, 'mitracairnewperbulan'));
      sumScoreNew += window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idxScoreNew] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scoremitracairnewperbulan'));
      totPlfRen += window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idxRen] || window.getCellValue(r, 'renewalmitracairperbulan'));
      sumScoreRen += window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idxScoreRen] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerenewalmitracairperbulan'));
      sumRep0 += window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idxRep0] || window.getCellValue(r, 'repaymentratedpd0'));
      sumScore0 += window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idxScore0] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentratedpd0'));
      sumScore130 += window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idxScore130] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentrate130daritunggakan'));
      sumScore3190 += window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idxScore3190] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentrate3190'));
    });

    cards = [
      { label: 'Plafond Mitra Cair New', val: 'Rp ' + totPlfNew.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'MC', note: 'Total Plafond' },
      { label: 'Score Mitra Cair New', val: (sumScoreNew / lenK).toFixed(1), icon: 'S1', note: 'Rata-rata Score' },
      { label: 'Plafond Renewal Mitra', val: 'Rp ' + totPlfRen.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'RN', note: 'Total Plafond' },
      { label: 'Score Renewal Mitra', val: (sumScoreRen / lenK).toFixed(1), icon: 'S2', note: 'Rata-rata Score' },
      { label: '% Repayment Rate DPD 0', val: (sumRep0 / lenK).toFixed(1) + '%', icon: 'R0', note: 'Rata-rata %' },
      { label: 'Score Repayment DPD 0', val: (sumScore0 / lenK).toFixed(1), icon: 'S3', note: 'Rata-rata Score' },
      { label: 'Score Repayment DPD 1-30', val: (sumScore130 / lenK).toFixed(1), icon: 'S4', note: 'Rata-rata Score' },
      { label: 'Score Repayment DPD 31-90', val: (sumScore3190 / lenK).toFixed(1), icon: 'S5', note: 'Rata-rata Score' }
    ];

  // 5. Highlight Card KPI BP
  } else if (key === 'kpi') {
    var totMitraNew = 0, sumScoreNewBp = 0, totMitraRen = 0, sumScoreRenBp = 0;
    var sumRep0Bp = 0, sumScore0Bp = 0, sumScore130Bp = 0, sumScore3190Bp = 0;
    var lenBp = data.length || 1;

    data.forEach(function(r) {
      totMitraNew += window.numVal(r['_raw_9'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'mitracairnewperbulan'));
      sumScoreNewBp += window.numVal(r['_raw_11'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scoremitracairnewperbulan'));
      totMitraRen += window.numVal(r['_raw_13'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'renewalmitracairperbulan'));
      sumScoreRenBp += window.numVal(r['_raw_15'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerenewalmitracairperbulan'));
      sumRep0Bp += window.numVal(r['_raw_17'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'repaymentratedpd0'));
      sumScore0Bp += window.numVal(r['_raw_19'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentratedpd0'));
      sumScore130Bp += window.numVal(r['_raw_23'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentrate130daritunggakan'));
      sumScore3190Bp += window.numVal(r['_raw_27'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentrate3190'));
    });

    cards = [
      { label: 'Jumlah Mitra Cair New', val: totMitraNew.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'MC', note: 'Total Mitra' },
      { label: 'Score Mitra Cair New', val: (sumScoreNewBp / lenBp).toFixed(1), icon: 'S1', note: 'Rata-rata Score' },
      { label: 'Total Renewal Mitra', val: totMitraRen.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'RN', note: 'Total Mitra' },
      { label: 'Score Renewal Mitra', val: (sumScoreRenBp / lenBp).toFixed(1), icon: 'S2', note: 'Rata-rata Score' },
      { label: '% Repayment Rate DPD 0', val: (sumRep0Bp / lenBp).toFixed(1) + '%', icon: 'R0', note: 'Rata-rata %' },
      { label: 'Score Repayment DPD 0', val: (sumScore0Bp / lenBp).toFixed(1), icon: 'S3', note: 'Rata-rata Score' },
      { label: 'Score Repayment DPD 1-30', val: (sumScore130Bp / lenBp).toFixed(1), icon: 'S4', note: 'Rata-rata Score' },
      { label: 'Score Repayment DPD 31-90', val: (sumScore3190Bp / lenBp).toFixed(1), icon: 'S5', note: 'Rata-rata Score' }
    ];

  // 6. Highlight Card Pencapaian Area / Point / BP
  } else if (key === 'pencapaianArea' || key === 'pencapaianPoint' || key === 'pencapaian') {
    var calculationRows = data;
    if (key === 'pencapaianArea') {
      var seenAreas = {};
      calculationRows = [];
      data.forEach(function(row) {
        var arName = String(window.getCellValue(row, 'area') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (arName && !seenAreas[arName]) {
          seenAreas[arName] = true;
          calculationRows.push(row);
        }
      });
    }

    var sumActualNTB = 0, sumActualETB = 0, sumMitra = 0, sumTotalDisb = 0;
    var sumTarget = 0, sumAchNTB = 0, sumAchETB = 0;

    calculationRows.forEach(function(r) {
      sumActualNTB += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'actualntb'));
      sumActualETB += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'actualetb'));
      sumMitra += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'jumlahmitra'));
      sumTotalDisb += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'totaldisb') || window.getCellValue(r, 'totalplafond'));
      sumTarget += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'target') || window.getCellValue(r, 'targettotal'));
      sumAchNTB += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'achievmentntb'));
      sumAchETB += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'achievmentetb'));
    });

    var lenP = calculationRows.length || 1;
    var avgAchNTB = (sumAchNTB / lenP).toFixed(1) + '%';
    var avgAchETB = (sumAchETB / lenP).toFixed(1) + '%';
    var achTotalVal = sumTarget > 0 ? ((sumTotalDisb / sumTarget) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';

    var rankVal = '-';
    if (calculationRows.length > 0) {
      var firstRank = window.getCellValue(calculationRows[0], 'ranking');
      if (firstRank && firstRank !== '-') {
        rankVal = '#' + firstRank;
      }
    }

    cards = [
      { label: 'ACTUAL NTB', val: sumActualNTB.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'NTB', note: 'Total Actual NTB' },
      { label: 'ACTUAL ETB', val: sumActualETB.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'ETB', note: 'Total Actual ETB' },
      { label: 'Jumlah Mitra', val: sumMitra.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'MT', note: 'Total Mitra' },
      { label: 'Total Disb', val: 'Rp ' + sumTotalDisb.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'DS', note: 'Total Disb' },
      { label: 'ACHIEVMENT NTB', val: avgAchNTB, icon: 'A1', note: 'Rata-rata %' },
      { label: 'ACHIEVMENT ETB', val: avgAchETB, icon: 'A2', note: 'Rata-rata %' },
      { label: 'ACHIEVMENT TOTAL', val: achTotalVal, icon: 'AT', note: 'Total Actual ÷ Total Target' },
      { label: 'Ranking', val: rankVal, icon: 'RK', note: (calculationRows.length === 1 ? 'Peringkat Terpilih' : 'Sesuai Filter') }
    ];

  // 7. Highlight Card Sosialisasi
  } else if (key === 'sosialisasi') {
    var totSos = data.length;
    var sosAman = 0, sosPalsu = 0;
    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var ket = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'keterangan') || r['_raw_12'] || r['_raw_7'] || '').toLowerCase();
      if (ket.indexOf('palsu') !== -1 || ket.indexOf('potensi') !== -1) {
        sosPalsu++;
      } else {
        sosAman++;
      }
    });
    cards = [
      { label: 'Jumlah Calon Mitra SOS', val: totSos.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'S', note: 'Sesuai filter' },
      { label: 'SOS Aman', val: sosAman.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: '✓', note: 'Berdasarkan Keterangan' },
      { label: 'SOS Potensi Palsu', val: sosPalsu.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: '✕', note: 'Berdasarkan Keterangan' }
    ];

  // 8. Highlight Card Penagihan & Kolektabilitas
  } else if (['newPar', 'dpd830', 'dpd3160', 'dpd6190', 'wob'].indexOf(key) !== -1) {
    var totMitraVisit = data.length;
    var totNominalCollect = 0;
    data.forEach(function(r) {
      totNominalCollect += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'nominalcollect') || r['_raw_7'] || r['_raw_10']);
    });

    cards = [
      { label: 'Jumlah Mitra Visit', val: totMitraVisit.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'MV', note: 'Sesuai filter' },
      { label: 'Jumlah Collect', val: 'Rp ' + totNominalCollect.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'Rp', note: 'Total nominal collect' }
    ];

  // 9. Highlight Card Validasi UK / Modal BM
  } else if (key === 'validasi') {
    var majelisSetVal = {};
    var totMitraDiVisit = data.length;
    var totMitraValidasi = 0;
    var validKeywords = ['done vuk', 'done', 'selesai', 'sesuai', 'berhasil', 'aprove', 'aproval'];

    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var majName = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'majelis') || r['_raw_5'] || '').trim();
      if (majName) majelisSetVal[majName.toLowerCase()] = true;

      var hasil = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'hasilvisit') || window.getCellValue(r, 'keterangan') || r['_raw_7'] || '').toLowerCase();
      var isValid = validKeywords.some(function(kw) { return hasil.indexOf(kw) !== -1; });
      if (isValid) totMitraValidasi++;
    });

    var pctVal = totMitraDiVisit > 0 ? ((totMitraValidasi / totMitraDiVisit) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';

    cards = [
      { label: 'Total Majelis', val: Object.keys(majelisSetVal).length, icon: 'MJ', note: 'Majelis unik terinput' },
      { label: 'Jumlah Mitra di-Visit', val: totMitraDiVisit.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'MV', note: 'Akumulasi total mitra' },
      { label: 'Jumlah Mitra Validasi', val: totMitraValidasi.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: '✓', note: 'Mitra berstatus valid' },
      { label: 'Persentase Validasi', val: pctVal, icon: '%', note: 'Mitra valid ÷ total mitra' }
    ];

  // 10. Highlight Card Suprise Visit
  } else if (key === 'suprise') {
    var majSetSup = {};
    var totMitraSup = 0;
    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var mSup = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'majelis') || r['_raw_4'] || '').trim();
      if (mSup) majSetSup[mSup.toLowerCase()] = true;
      totMitraSup += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'jumlahmitra') || r['_raw_6']);
    });
    var countMajSup = Object.keys(majSetSup).length;
    var avgMitraSup = countMajSup > 0 ? (totMitraSup / countMajSup).toFixed(1) : '0';

    cards = [
      { label: 'Jumlah Majelis', val: countMajSup, icon: 'MJ', note: 'Jumlah inputan majelis' },
      { label: 'Jumlah Mitra', val: totMitraSup, icon: 'MT', note: 'Dari tabel Jumlah Mitra' },
      { label: 'Rata-rata Mitra / Majelis', val: avgMitraSup, icon: 'MD', note: 'Total mitra ÷ jumlah majelis' }
    ];

  // 11. Highlight Card Pelayanan
  } else if (key === 'pelayanan') {
    var majelisKumpulSet = {}, majelisSemiSet = {}, majelisD2DSet = {}, allMajelisSet = {};
    var mitMK = 0, mitMS = 0, mitD2D = 0;

    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var jkm = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'jeniskumpulanmajelis') || '').toLowerCase();
      var majName = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'majelis') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var jm = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'jumlahmitra'));

      if (jkm.indexOf('semi') !== -1) {
        if (majName) majelisSemiSet[majName] = true;
        mitMS += jm;
      } else if (jkm.indexOf('dor') !== -1 || jkm.indexOf('door') !== -1 || jkm.indexOf('d2d') !== -1) {
        if (majName) majelisD2DSet[majName] = true;
        mitD2D += jm;
      } else {
        if (majName) majelisKumpulSet[majName] = true;
        mitMK += jm;
      }

      if (majName) allMajelisSet[majName] = true;
    });

    var countMK = Object.keys(majelisKumpulSet).length;
    var countMS = Object.keys(majelisSemiSet).length;
    var countD2D = Object.keys(majelisD2DSet).length;
    var totalMajelisUnik = Object.keys(allMajelisSet).length;

    cards = [
      { label: 'Jumlah Majelis Kumpul', val: countMK, icon: 'MK', note: 'Majelis unik (tanpa duplikat)' },
      { label: 'Jumlah Majelis Semi Kumpul', val: countMS, icon: 'MS', note: 'Majelis unik (tanpa duplikat)' },
      { label: 'Jumlah Majelis Dor To Dor', val: countD2D, icon: 'D2D', note: 'Majelis unik (tanpa duplikat)' },
      { label: 'Total Mitra Majelis Kumpul', val: mitMK, icon: 'MTK', note: 'Akumulasi total mitra' },
      { label: 'Total Mitra Majelis Semi Kumpul', val: mitMS, icon: 'MTS', note: 'Akumulasi total mitra' },
      { label: 'Total Mitra Majelis Dor To Dor', val: mitD2D, icon: 'MTD', note: 'Akumulasi total mitra' },
      { label: 'Total Majelis', val: totalMajelisUnik, icon: 'TM', note: 'Semua jenis majelis unik' },
      { label: 'Total Mitra', val: (mitMK + mitMS + mitD2D), icon: 'MT', note: 'Semua jenis majelis' }
    ];

  // 12. Highlight Card Kunjungan Eligible & DO (Dengan Perlindungan Zero-ID Guard)
  } else if (key === 'kunjunganWL' || key === 'kunjunganDO') {
    var seenMitraVisit = {};
    var vTotal = 0, vMinat = 0, vTdkMinat = 0;

    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var rawId = window.getCellValue(r, 'idloan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'id_loan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'loan_id') || '';
      var cleanId = window.normalizeNumericId(rawId);
      var namaMitra = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'namamitra') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama_mitra') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var mitraKey = cleanId ? ('ID_' + cleanId) : (namaMitra ? ('NM_' + namaMitra) : ('ROW_' + Math.random()));

      if (seenMitraVisit[mitraKey]) return;
      seenMitraVisit[mitraKey] = true;
      vTotal++;

      var rawKet = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'keterangan') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var rawHasil = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'hasilvisit') || '').trim().toLowerCase();

      var isMinat = false;
      if (rawKet) {
        isMinat = (rawKet === 'minat' || (rawKet.indexOf('minat') !== -1 && rawKet.indexOf('tidak') === -1 && rawKet.indexOf('belum') === -1 && rawKet.indexOf('blm') === -1));
      } else {
        isMinat = (rawHasil.indexOf('minat') !== -1 && rawHasil.indexOf('tidak') === -1 && rawHasil.indexOf('belum') === -1 && rawHasil.indexOf('blm') === -1);
      }

      if (isMinat) vMinat++;
      else vTdkMinat++;
    });

    cards = [
      { label: 'Jumlah Kunjungan (Mitra Unik)', val: vTotal, icon: 'K', note: 'Tanpa duplikasi mitra (Zero-ID Safe)' },
      { label: 'Mitra Minat', val: vMinat, icon: '+', note: 'Sinkron Kolom Keterangan' },
      { label: 'Mitra Tidak Minat', val: vTdkMinat, icon: '-', note: 'Sinkron Kolom Keterangan' }
    ];

  // 13. Highlight Card KP Daily (Aproval Perhari)
  } else if (key === 'approvalPerhari') {
    var custUnikMap = {};
    var sumPlafondUnik = 0, groupLoanCount = 0, modalCount = 0, newMitraCount = 0, lanjutanMitraCount = 0;
    data.forEach(function(r) {
      var cNum = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'customer_number') || window.getCellValue(r, 'customernumber') || '').trim();
      var pNominal = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'plafond'));
      var pType = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'product_type')).toLowerCase();
      var kCycle = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'kategori_cycle')).toLowerCase();

      if (cNum && !custUnikMap[cNum]) {
        custUnikMap[cNum] = true; sumPlafondUnik += pNominal;
      }
      if (pType.indexOf('grub') !== -1 || pType.indexOf('group') !== -1) groupLoanCount++; else if (pType.indexOf('modal') !== -1) modalCount++;
      if (kCycle.indexOf('subsequent') !== -1 || kCycle.indexOf('lanjutan') !== -1) lanjutanMitraCount++; else newMitraCount++;
    });
    cards = [
      { label: 'Total Mitra Unik', val: Object.keys(custUnikMap).length, icon: 'TM', note: 'Berdasarkan CUSTOMER_NUMBER' },
      { label: 'Total Plafond Unik', val: 'Rp ' + sumPlafondUnik.toLocaleString('id-ID'), icon: 'PL', note: 'Plafond tanpa duplikat' },
      { label: 'Product Group Loan', val: groupLoanCount, icon: 'GL', note: 'Grub Loan' },
      { label: 'Product Modal', val: modalCount, icon: 'PM', note: 'Modal' },
      { label: 'Mitra Baru (New)', val: newMitraCount, icon: 'MN', note: 'Cycle Baru' },
      { label: 'Mitra Lanjutan', val: lanjutanMitraCount, icon: 'ML', note: 'Subsequent Mitra' }
    ];

  } else if (['loanPaidPoint', 'loanPaidArea', 'loanPaidRegional', 'cohort7', 'renewal', 'reactivation'].indexOf(key) !== -1) {
    cards = [];
  } else {
    cards = [{ label: 'Total Inputan Data', val: data.length, icon: 'D', note: 'Sesuai filter' }];
  }

  statsContainer.innerHTML = cards.map(function(c) {
    return '<div class="stat-card">' +
      '<div class="stat-icon">' + c.icon + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stat-label">' + c.label + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stat-value">' + c.val + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stat-note">' + c.note + '</div>' +
    '</div>';
  }).join('');
};

window.initDraggableAssistant = function() {
  var btn = document.getElementById('assistantWidgetBtn');
  if (!btn) return;

  var isDragging = false;
  var startX, startY, initialLeft, initialTop;
  var hasMoved = false;

  var savedPos = localStorage.getItem('asistan_pos');
  if (savedPos) {
    try {
      var pos = JSON.parse(savedPos);
      if (pos.left !== undefined && pos.top !== undefined) {
        btn.style.left = pos.left + 'px';
        btn.style.top = pos.top + 'px';
        btn.style.right = 'auto';
        btn.style.bottom = 'auto';
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  var onStart = function(e) {
    var clientX = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
    var clientY = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    isDragging = true;
    hasMoved = false;
    startX = clientX;
    startY = clientY;

    var rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
    initialLeft = rect.left;
    initialTop = rect.top;

    btn.style.transition = 'none';
  };

  var onMove = function(e) {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    var clientX = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
    var clientY = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    var dx = clientX - startX;
    var dy = clientY - startY;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > 4 || Math.abs(dy) > 4) {
      hasMoved = true;
    }

    var newLeft = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 65, initialLeft + dx));
    var newTop = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 65, initialTop + dy));

    btn.style.left = newLeft + 'px';
    btn.style.top = newTop + 'px';
    btn.style.right = 'auto';
    btn.style.bottom = 'auto';
  };

  var onEnd = function(e) {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    isDragging = false;
    btn.style.transition = 'transform 0.2s';

    var rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
    localStorage.setItem('asistan_pos', JSON.stringify({ left: Math.round(rect.left), top: Math.round(rect.top) }));

    if (!hasMoved) {
      if (typeof window.toggleAssistantModal === 'function') {
        window.toggleAssistantModal();
      }
    }
  };

  btn.addEventListener('mousedown', onStart);
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', onEnd);

  btn.addEventListener('touchstart', onStart, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: false });
  window.addEventListener('touchend', onEnd);
};

// ============================================================================
// [BATAS AKHIR PART 1 - SAMBUNGKAN KODE DARI PART 2 TEPAT DI BAWAH BARIS INI]
// ============================================================================

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * FRONTEND ENGINE - AKTIVITAS REGIONAL GORONTALO (PART 2 DARI 3 - REVISED V13)
 * ============================================================================
 * Bagian 2 memuat:
 * 1. Column Definition Mapper Presisi & Dynamic Header Resolver (Renewal & Reactivation).
 * 2. Main Table Renderer & Pagination (Zero-ID Safety Guard Anti-Visit 100+).
 * 3. Cohort 7+ Latest Record WOB (Status Kunjungan & Bukti Foto Mutakhir).
 * 4. Cross-Matching ID Loan/Cust Number & Bukti Foto Post Disb di KP Daily.
 * 5. Universal Photo Viewer Button ("📷 Lihat Foto" di Semua Menu).
 * 6. Truncation Teks Hasil Visit (2 Baris) & Pop-up Modal Detail Ungu.
 * 7. Loan Paid Presisi Teks Wilayah (Bukan 0.0%) & Komparasi Delta Daily vs W1.
 * 8. Matriks Approval Reaktif Filter Point, Area, dan BP.
 * 9. Performa KP Per Week Day-to-Day Engine (Filter Tab Hari dengan Dot Hijau).
 * 10. Review Performa Pencapaian (Top 5 / Bottom 5 Point & Top 10 / Bottom 10 BP).
 * 11. Chart.js Combo Engine (KPI Multi-Score AM/RM/BM/BP, 7 Red Cards, Dashboard Donut & Line).
 * 12. Summary Inputan Per BP & Summary Harian BP Lapangan Berwarna Reaktif Filter.
 */

window.getDisplayColumns = function(key, isLoggedIn) {

      { label: 'HASIL KUNJUNGAN TERBARU', key: 'hasil_kunjungan', rawIdx: -1 },
      { label: 'BUKTI FOTO WOB', key: 'bukti_foto_kunjungan', rawIdx: -1 }
