    cols = [
      { label: 'STATUS KUNJUNGAN', key: 'status_kunjungan', isSyncCol: true },
      { label: 'STATUS MINAT', key: 'status_minat', isSyncCol: true },
      { label: (key === 'renewal' ? 'BUKTI FOTO ELIGIBLE' : 'BUKTI FOTO DO'), key: 'dokumentasifoto', isSyncCol: true }
    ];

    var rawHeaders = window.currentHeaders || [];
    var excludedKeys = ['status_kunjungan', 'status_minat', 'dokumentasifoto', 'is_visited', 'matching_type', 'bukti_foto', 'bukti_foto_kunjungan', 'hasil_kunjungan'];

    if (rawHeaders.length > 0) {
      rawHeaders.forEach(function(h, idx) {
        var cleanH = String(h || '').trim();
        var lowerH = cleanH.toLowerCase();
        var isExcluded = excludedKeys.some(function(ex) { return lowerH === ex || lowerH.indexOf('_raw_') === 0; });
        if (!isExcluded && cleanH) {
          cols.push({
            label: cleanH.toUpperCase(),
            key: cleanH,
            rawIdx: idx,
            isImported: true
          });
        }
      });
    } else if (window.filteredData && window.filteredData.length > 0) {
      var sampleRow = window.filteredData[0];
      Object.keys(sampleRow).forEach(function(rk) {
        var cleanKey = String(rk || '').trim();
        var lowerKey = cleanKey.toLowerCase();
        var isExcluded2 = excludedKeys.some(function(ex2) { return lowerKey === ex2 || lowerKey.indexOf('_raw_') === 0; });
        if (!isExcluded2 && cleanKey) {
          cols.push({
            label: cleanKey.replace(/_/g, ' ').toUpperCase(),
            key: cleanKey,
            isImported: true
          });
        }
      });
    } else {
      cols.push(
        { label: 'CUSTOMER NUMBER', key: 'customer_number' },
        { label: 'LOAN ID', key: 'loan_id' },
        { label: 'NAMA MITRA', key: 'customer_name' },
        { label: 'AREA', key: 'area' },
        { label: 'POINT', key: 'point' },
        { label: 'PLAFOND', key: 'plafond' }
      );
    }
  } else {
    if (window.currentHeaders && window.currentHeaders.length > 0) {
      cols = window.currentHeaders.map(function(h, idx) {
        return { label: h, key: window.canonicalHeader ? window.canonicalHeader(h) : ('col_' + idx), rawIdx: idx };
      });
    } else {
      cols = [
        { label: 'TANGGAL', key: 'tanggal', rawIdx: 0 },
        { label: 'BP / BM', key: 'bpbm', rawIdx: 1 },
        { label: 'POINT', key: 'point', rawIdx: 2 },
        { label: 'AREA', key: 'area', rawIdx: 3 },
        { label: 'AKTIVITAS', key: 'aktivitas', rawIdx: 4 },
        { label: 'HASIL VISIT', key: 'hasilvisit', rawIdx: 8 },
        { label: 'KETERANGAN', key: 'keterangan', rawIdx: 9 }
      ];
    }
  }

  return cols;
};

window.renderTable = function() {
  var tHead = document.getElementById('tableHead');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
  var rCount = document.getElementById('rowCount');
  var dupBanner = document.getElementById('duplicateBanner');
  var dupText = document.getElementById('duplicateBannerText');
  var btnDup = document.getElementById('btnFilterDuplicate');
  if (!tHead || !tBody) return;

  var dataset = (window.currentKey === 'postDisb') ? (window.postDisbList || []) : (window.filteredData || []);
  if (rCount) rCount.textContent = dataset.length + ' data ditemukan';

  // REVISI: Zero-ID Safety Guard Anti-Visit 100+
  window.visitIndexMap = {};
  window.mitraTotalVisitsMap = {};

  if (window.currentKey === 'kunjunganWL' || window.currentKey === 'kunjunganDO') {
    var mitraTimeline = {};
    var nameFuzzyBuckets = [];

    dataset.forEach(function(r) {
      var rawId = window.getCellValue(r, 'idloan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'loan_id') || r['_raw_5'] || r['_raw_4'] || '';
      var cleanId = window.normalizeNumericId(rawId);
      var rawName = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'namamitra') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || r['_raw_6'] || '').trim();

      var mKey = '';
      if (cleanId) {
        mKey = 'ID_' + cleanId;
      } else if (rawName && rawName !== '-' && rawName.toLowerCase() !== 'undefined') {
        // Fallback Fuzzy Matching Nama jika ID Loan kosong atau bernilai '0'
        var matchedBucketKey = null;
        for (var b = 0; b < nameFuzzyBuckets.length; b++) {
          if (window.calculateNameSimilarity(nameFuzzyBuckets[b].name, rawName) >= 0.90) {
            matchedBucketKey = nameFuzzyBuckets[b].key;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (matchedBucketKey) {
          mKey = matchedBucketKey;
        } else {
          mKey = 'NAME_' + rawName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
          nameFuzzyBuckets.push({ name: rawName, key: mKey });
        }
      }

      if (!mKey) return;

      var dStr = window.getCellValue(r, 'tanggal') || r['_raw_9'] || '';
      var tStr = window.getCellValue(r, 'waktu') || r['_raw_10'] || '';
      var pDate = window.parseDateStr(dStr);
      var timeMillis = pDate ? pDate.getTime() : 0;

      if (!mitraTimeline[mKey]) mitraTimeline[mKey] = [];
      mitraTimeline[mKey].push({ row: r, time: timeMillis, timeStr: tStr });
    });

    var dupMitraCount = 0;
    var dupInputanTotal = 0;

    Object.keys(mitraTimeline).forEach(function(mKey) {
      var arr = mitraTimeline[mKey];
      arr.sort(function(a, b) { return a.time - b.time; });

      window.mitraTotalVisitsMap[mKey] = arr.length;
      if (arr.length > 1) {
        dupMitraCount++;
        dupInputanTotal += arr.length;
      }

      arr.forEach(function(item, idx) {
        item.row._visitOrderIndex = idx + 1;
        item.row._mitraKey = mKey;
      });
    });

    if (dupBanner) {
      if (dupMitraCount > 0) {
        dupBanner.style.display = 'flex';
        if (dupText) dupText.textContent = 'Terdeteksi ' + dupMitraCount + ' Mitra dikunjungi berulang (Total ' + dupInputanTotal + ' inputan).';
        if (btnDup) {
          btnDup.textContent = window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly ? 'Tampilkan Semua Data' : 'Lihat Data Duplikat';
          btnDup.style.background = window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly ? '#15803d' : '#ea580c';
        }
      } else {
        dupBanner.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }
  } else if (dupBanner) {
    dupBanner.style.display = 'none';
  }

  var cols = window.getDisplayColumns(window.currentKey, !!window.currentUser);
  var isDynamicImport = (window.currentKey === 'renewal' || window.currentKey === 'reactivation');
  
  tHead.innerHTML = '<tr>' + cols.map(function(c) {
    var thClass = c.isImported ? ' class="th-import-header"' : (c.isSyncCol ? ' style="background:#eff6ff;color:#1e40af;font-weight:850;"' : '');
    return '<th' + thClass + '>' + c.label + '</th>';
  }).join('') + '</tr>';

  if (!dataset || dataset.length === 0) {
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="' + Math.max(1, cols.length) + '" class="empty">Tidak ada data yang sesuai filter.</td></tr>';
    window.renderPaginationControls(0, 0, 0);
    return;
  }

  var totalRows = dataset.length;
  var pageSizeNum = (window.pageSize === 'all') ? totalRows : window.pageSize;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / pageSizeNum) || 1;
  if (window.currentPage > totalPages) window.currentPage = totalPages;

  var startIdx = (window.currentPage - 1) * pageSizeNum;
  var endIdx = Math.min(startIdx + pageSizeNum, totalRows);
  var pageRows = dataset.slice(startIdx, endIdx);

  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
  var isLoanPaid = ['loanPaidPoint', 'loanPaidArea', 'loanPaidRegional'].indexOf(window.currentKey) !== -1;

  var htmlRows = pageRows.map(function(row) {
    var trClasses = [];
    if (window.currentKey === 'approvalPerhari') {
      var plfNum = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(row, 'plafond') || row['_raw_7']);
      var stCair = String(window.getCellValue(row, 'status_cair') || row['_raw_14'] || '').toLowerCase();
      if (plfNum > 0 && plfNum <= 4000000 && stCair.indexOf('menunggu') !== -1) {
        trClasses.push('tr-plafond-warning');
      }
    }

    var trClassAttr = trClasses.length > 0 ? (' class="' + trClasses.join(' ') + '"') : '';
    var cellsHtml = cols.map(function(col) {
      return window.renderCellContent(row, col, role, isLoanPaid);
    }).join('');

    return '<tr' + trClassAttr + '>' + cellsHtml + '</tr>';
  });

  tBody.innerHTML = htmlRows.join('');
  window.renderPaginationControls(totalRows, startIdx + 1, endIdx);
};

window.renderCellContent = function(row, col, userRole, isLoanPaid) {
  var key = col.key;
  var val = window.getCellValue(row, key, col.rawIdx);

  // Aksi Hapus Baris untuk Sheet Inputan
  if (key === 'action_delete') {
    return '<td><button type="button" class="action-delete-btn" onclick="window.confirmDeleteHasilInputan(' + JSON.stringify(row).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')">Hapus</button></td>';
  }

  // Kolom Aksi Verifikasi Post Disb
  if (key === 'action_verify') {
    var pStatus = String(row.status_post_disb || '').trim();
    var pVerifier = String(row.verified_by || '').trim();
    var canVerify = (userRole === '1-RM' || userRole === '2-AM' || userRole === '3-BM' || userRole === 'Editor' || userRole === 'Admin');

    if (!canVerify) return '<td><span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:10px;">Akses Terbatas</span></td>';

    if (pStatus === 'Sudah Post Disb') {
      return '<td>' +
        '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;">' +
          '<button type="button" class="badge-post-sudah" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="window.openPostDisbVerifyModal(' + JSON.stringify(row).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')">✓ ' + (pVerifier || 'Terverifikasi') + '</button>' +
          '<button type="button" class="action-delete-btn" style="padding:2px 6px;font-size:9.5px;" onclick="window.cancelPostDisbVerification(' + JSON.stringify(row).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')" title="Batalkan Verifikasi">Batal</button>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</td>';
    } else if (pStatus === 'Tidak Direspon') {
      return '<td>' +
        '<button type="button" class="badge-post-no-response" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="window.openPostDisbVerifyModal(' + JSON.stringify(row).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')">🟡 Update: Tidak Direspon</button>' +
      '</td>';
    } else if (pStatus === 'Tidak Bisa Dihubungi') {
      return '<td>' +
        '<button type="button" class="badge-post-unreachable" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="window.openPostDisbVerifyModal(' + JSON.stringify(row).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')">🔴 Update: Nomor Mati</button>' +
      '</td>';
    } else {
      return '<td>' +
        '<button type="button" class="auth-btn" style="padding:4px 10px;font-size:10px;background:#16a34a;" onclick="window.openPostDisbVerifyModal(' + JSON.stringify(row).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')">Verifikasi</button>' +
      '</td>';
    }
  }

  // REVISI: Bukti Foto Post Disb di Menu KP Daily (approvalPerhari)
  if (key === 'bukti_post_disb') {
    var rawProof = String(val || row.bukti_post_disb || row.bukti_url || '').trim();
    if (!rawProof || rawProof === '-' || rawProof.toLowerCase() === 'undefined') {
      return '<td><span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:10px;">-</span></td>';
    }
    var cleanProofUrl = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(rawProof);
    var pName = (row.customer_name || row.customer_number || 'Mitra');
    return '<td>' +
      '<button type="button" class="table-photo-btn" onclick="window.openPhotoViewerModal(\'' + cleanProofUrl + '\', \'Bukti Post Disb: ' + String(pName).replace(/'/g, "\\'") + '\')">' +
        '<span>📷</span> <span>Bukti Post Disb</span>' +
      '</button>' +
    '</td>';
  }

  if (key === 'status_post_disb') {
    var stPost = String(val || row.status_post_disb || 'Belum Post Disb').trim();
    var verInfo = String(row.verified_by || row.post_disb_verifier || '').trim();

    if (stPost === 'Sudah Post Disb') {
      return '<td>' +
        '<span class="badge-post-sudah">🟢 Sudah Post Disb</span>' +
        (verInfo ? ('<span class="badge-post-verifier">Oleh: ' + verInfo + '</span>') : '') +
      '</td>';
    } else if (stPost === 'Tidak Direspon') {
      return '<td><span class="badge-post-no-response">🟡 Tidak Direspon</span></td>';
    } else if (stPost === 'Tidak Bisa Dihubungi') {
      return '<td><span class="badge-post-unreachable">🔴 Tidak Bisa Dihubungi</span></td>';
    } else {
      return '<td><span class="badge-post-belum">⚪ Belum Post Disb</span></td>';
    }
  }

  if (key === 'post_disb_verifier') {
    var vText = String(val || row.verified_by || '-').trim();
    return '<td><strong style="color:#0284c7;font-size:10.5px;">' + (vText || '-') + '</strong></td>';
  }

  // REVISI: Status Kunjungan Cross-Match (Cohort 7+, Renewal, Reactivation)
  if (key === 'status_kunjungan') {
    var isVisited = !!row.is_visited || (String(val).toLowerCase().indexOf('sudah') !== -1);
    var matchType = row.matching_type || '';
    var matchBadge = '';
    if (matchType === 'id_match') {
      matchBadge = ' <span class="badge-match-indicator badge-match-id">✓ ID Match</span>';
    } else if (matchType === 'name_match') {
      matchBadge = ' <span class="badge-match-indicator badge-match-name">≈ Fuzzy 90%</span>';
    }

    if (isVisited) {
      return '<td><span class="badge-cross-visited">🟢 Sudah Dikunjungi' + matchBadge + '</span></td>';
    } else {
      return '<td><span class="badge-cross-unvisited">⚪ Belum Dikunjungi</span></td>';
    }
  }

  // REVISI: Status Minat Cross-Match (Renewal & Reactivation)
  if (key === 'status_minat') {
    var stMinat = String(val || row.status_minat || '-').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (stMinat.indexOf('minat') !== -1 && stMinat.indexOf('tidak') === -1) {
      return '<td><span class="badge-cross-minat">🟢 Minat</span></td>';
    } else if (stMinat.indexOf('tidak') !== -1) {
      return '<td><span class="badge-cross-tidak-minat">🔴 Tidak Minat</span></td>';
    }
    return '<td><span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:10px;">-</span></td>';
  }

  // Kolom Status Validasi KP Daily
  if (key === 'status_validasi') {
    var stVal = String(val || row.status_validasi || 'Belum Validasi').trim();
    var isGl = !!row.is_group_loan;
    var fUrl = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(row.foto_validasi || '');

    if (stVal === 'Sudah Validasi') {
      return '<td>' +
        '<span class="badge-validasi-sudah">🟢 Sudah Validasi' + (isGl ? ' (GL Auto)' : '') + '</span>' +
        (fUrl ? (' <button type="button" class="visit-more-link" onclick="window.openPhotoViewerModal(\'' + fUrl + '\', \'Bukti Validasi: ' + (row.customer_name || '-') + '\')">📷 Foto</button>') : '') +
      '</td>';
    } else {
      return '<td><span class="badge-validasi-belum">🔴 Belum Validasi</span></td>';
    }
  }

  // Kolom Nomor Telepon dengan tombol aksi hubungi
  if (key === 'phone_no' || key === 'no_hp') {
    var cleanPhone = window.formatWhatsAppNumber(val);
    if (!cleanPhone) return '<td>-</td>';
    return '<td>' +
      '<button type="button" class="phone-action-btn" onclick="window.openContactMitraModal(' + JSON.stringify(row).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')" title="Hubungi via WA / Telepon">' +
        '<span>📞</span> <span>' + val + '</span>' +
      '</button>' +
    '</td>';
  }

  // Kolom Nama Mitra dengan Badge Visit ke-X
  if (key === 'namamitra' || key === 'customer_name' || key === 'borrower') {
    var rawMitra = String(val || '-').trim();
    var mKey = row._mitraKey;
    var vOrder = row._visitOrderIndex;
    var totVisits = mKey ? (window.mitraTotalVisitsMap[mKey] || 0) : 0;

    var visitBadgeHtml = '';
    if (totVisits > 1 && vOrder) {
      visitBadgeHtml = ' <span class="badge-visit-count">Visit ke-' + vOrder + '</span>';
    }

    return '<td><strong>' + rawMitra + '</strong>' + visitBadgeHtml + '</td>';
  }

  // Tanggal dengan Badge [HARI INI] Universal di Semua Menu
  if (key === 'tanggal' || key === 'tgl' || col.label === 'TANGGAL' || key === 'disbursement_date' || key === 'tanggal_gd') {
    var dateStr = String(val || '').trim();
    if (!dateStr || dateStr === '-') return '<td>-</td>';
    var isTodayRow = window.isToday(dateStr);
    var todayBadgeHtml = isTodayRow ? '<span class="badge-pill success" style="margin-right:5px;font-size:9.5px;padding:2px 6px;">HARI INI</span>' : '';
    return '<td>' + todayBadgeHtml + '<span>' + dateStr + '</span></td>';
  }

  // REVISI: Semua Menu yang Menampilkan Foto Wajib Berupa Tombol "📷 Lihat Foto" Modal Frame
  var isPhotoCol = (
    key === 'dokumentasifoto' || key === 'foto' || key === 'bukti_url' ||
    key === 'bukti_foto_kunjungan' || col.label === 'DOKUMENTASI FOTO' ||
    col.label === 'BUKTI VERIFIKASI' || col.label === 'BUKTI FOTO WOB' ||
    col.label === 'BUKTI FOTO ELIGIBLE' || col.label === 'BUKTI FOTO DO'
  );

  if (isPhotoCol) {
    var rawPhotoUrl = String(val || '').trim();
    if (!rawPhotoUrl || rawPhotoUrl === '-' || rawPhotoUrl.toLowerCase() === 'undefined') {
      return '<td><span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:10px;">-</span></td>';
    }
    var cleanPhotoUrl = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(rawPhotoUrl);
    var pTitle = (row.customer_name || row.namamitra || row.bpbm || 'Dokumentasi Bukti Lapangan');
    return '<td>' +
      '<button type="button" class="table-photo-btn" onclick="window.openPhotoViewerModal(\'' + cleanPhotoUrl + '\', \'' + String(pTitle).replace(/'/g, "\\'") + '\')">' +
        '<span>📷</span> <span>Lihat Foto</span>' +
      '</button>' +
    '</td>';
  }

  // Status Cair Pill
  if (key === 'status_cair') {
    var sc = String(val || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (sc.indexOf('sudah cair') !== -1 || sc === 'cair') {
      return '<td><span class="badge-pill success">Sudah Cair</span></td>';
    } else if (sc.indexOf('menunggu') !== -1) {
      return '<td><span class="badge-pill warning">Menunggu Cair</span></td>';
    } else if (sc.indexOf('gagal') !== -1) {
      return '<td><span class="badge-pill danger">Gagal Cair</span></td>';
    }
    return '<td><span class="badge-pill info">' + (val || '-') + '</span></td>';
  }

  if (key === 'plafond' || key === 'totalplafond' || key === 'totaldisb' || key === 'principal_amount' || key === 'nominalcollect') {
    var numPlf = window.numVal(val);
    return '<td style="font-weight:750;">Rp ' + numPlf.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>';
  }

  if (isLoanPaid) {
    return window.renderLoanPaidCell(row, col, val);
  }

  // REVISI: Hasil Visit / Keterangan dengan Truncation & Tombol "🔍 Lihat Selengkapnya" Pop-up Ungu
  if (key === 'hasilvisit' || key === 'keterangan' || key === 'hasil_kunjungan') {
    var fullText = String(val || '').trim();
    if (!fullText || fullText === '-') return '<td>-</td>';
    var shortText = fullText.length > 55 ? (fullText.substring(0, 52) + '...') : fullText;
    return '<td class="hasil-visit-cell">' +
      '<div class="hasil-visit-truncate" title="' + fullText.replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '">' + shortText + '</div>' +
      (fullText.length > 55 ? ('<button type="button" class="visit-more-link" onclick="window.openVisitDetailModal(' + JSON.stringify(row).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')">🔍 Lihat selengkapnya</button>') : '') +
    '</td>';
  }

  return '<td>' + (val !== undefined && val !== null && val !== '' ? val : '-') + '</td>';
};

window.renderLoanPaidCell = function(row, col, rawVal) {
  var colLabel = String(col.label || '').toUpperCase();
  var rawIdx = col.rawIdx;

  // Presisi: Kolom Pulau, Region, Area, Branch WAJIB berupa teks murni, BUKAN 0.0%!
  var isTextRegion = (colLabel.indexOf('PULAU') !== -1) || 
                     (colLabel.indexOf('REGION') !== -1) || 
                     (colLabel.indexOf('AREA') !== -1) || 
                     (colLabel.indexOf('BRANCH') !== -1) || 
                     col.isTextOnly || 
                     (col.key === 'pulau' || col.key === 'regional' || col.key === 'area' || col.key === 'point');

  if (isTextRegion) {
    var textVal = String(rawVal || '').trim();
    if (textVal === '0' || textVal === '0.0%' || textVal === '') {
      textVal = row['_raw_' + rawIdx] || row[col.key] || '-';
    }
    return '<td style="font-weight:750;color:var(--text-main);white-space:nowrap;">' + (textVal || '-') + '</td>';
  }

  var numVal = window.numVal(rawVal);
  var valStr = numVal.toFixed(1) + '%';

  var isDpd0 = (colLabel.indexOf('DPD 0') !== -1 || col.key === 'dpd_0');
  var isDpd130 = (colLabel.indexOf('1-30') !== -1 || col.key === 'dpd_1_30');
  var isDpd3190 = (colLabel.indexOf('31-90') !== -1 || col.key === 'dpd_31_90');

  var targetClass = '';
  if (isDpd0) {
    targetClass = (numVal >= 98.0) ? 'lp-target-green' : (numVal >= 95.0 ? 'lp-target-yellow' : 'lp-target-red');
  } else if (isDpd130) {
    targetClass = (numVal <= 55.0) ? 'lp-target-green' : (numVal <= 65.0 ? 'lp-target-yellow' : 'lp-target-red');
  } else if (isDpd3190) {
    targetClass = (numVal <= 13.0) ? 'lp-target-green' : (numVal <= 20.0 ? 'lp-target-yellow' : 'lp-target-red');
  }

  var isDailyCol = (colLabel.indexOf('DAYLI') !== -1 || colLabel.indexOf('DAILY') !== -1 || colLabel.indexOf('AKTUAL') !== -1);
  var diffBadgeHtml = '';

  if (isDailyCol) {
    var w1Val = 0;
    if (isDpd0) w1Val = window.numVal(row['w1_dpd_0'] || row['_raw_6'] || row['_raw_5'] || row['W1 DPD 0']);
    else if (isDpd130) w1Val = window.numVal(row['w1_dpd_1_30'] || row['_raw_7'] || row['_raw_6'] || row['W1 DPD 1-30']);
    else if (isDpd3190) w1Val = window.numVal(row['w1_dpd_31_90'] || row['_raw_8'] || row['_raw_7'] || row['W1 DPD 31-90']);

    if (w1Val > 0) {
      var diff = numVal - w1Val;
      var diffFormatted = (diff >= 0 ? '+' : '') + diff.toFixed(1) + '%';

      if (isDpd0) {
        if (diff > 0.05) diffBadgeHtml = '<span class="lp-diff-badge lp-diff-up" title="Naik dibanding W1">▲ ' + diffFormatted + '</span>';
        else if (diff < -0.05) diffBadgeHtml = '<span class="lp-diff-badge lp-diff-down" title="Turun dibanding W1">▼ ' + diffFormatted + '</span>';
        else diffBadgeHtml = '<span class="lp-diff-badge lp-diff-same">― 0.0%</span>';
      } else {
        if (diff < -0.05) diffBadgeHtml = '<span class="lp-diff-badge lp-diff-up" title="Membaik (turun) dibanding W1">▲ ' + diffFormatted + '</span>';
        else if (diff > 0.05) diffBadgeHtml = '<span class="lp-diff-badge lp-diff-down" title="Memburuk (naik) dibanding W1">▼ ' + diffFormatted + '</span>';
        else diffBadgeHtml = '<span class="lp-diff-badge lp-diff-same">― 0.0%</span>';
      }
    }
  }

  return '<td>' +
    '<span class="' + (targetClass || '') + '">' + valStr + '</span>' +
    diffBadgeHtml +
  '</td>';
};

window.renderPaginationControls = function(totalRows, start, end) {
  var pInfo = document.getElementById('pagInfo');
  var pControls = document.getElementById('pagControls');
  if (!pInfo || !pControls) return;

  if (totalRows === 0) {
    pInfo.textContent = 'Menampilkan 0 dari 0 baris';
    pControls.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  pInfo.textContent = 'Menampilkan ' + start + ' - ' + end + ' dari total ' + totalRows + ' baris';

  var pageSizeNum = (window.pageSize === 'all') ? totalRows : window.pageSize;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / pageSizeNum) || 1;
  if (totalPages <= 1) {
    pControls.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  var btns = [];
  btns.push('<button type="button" class="pag-btn" onclick="window.goToPage(' + (window.currentPage - 1) + ')"' + (window.currentPage === 1 ? ' disabled' : '') + '>❮</button>');

  var startP = Math.max(1, window.currentPage - 2);
  var endP = Math.min(totalPages, window.currentPage + 2);

  for (var p = startP; p <= endP; p++) {
    btns.push('<button type="button" class="pag-btn' + (p === window.currentPage ? ' active' : '') + '" onclick="window.goToPage(' + p + ')">' + p + '</button>');
  }

  btns.push('<button type="button" class="pag-btn" onclick="window.goToPage(' + (window.currentPage + 1) + ')"' + (window.currentPage === totalPages ? ' disabled' : '') + '>❯</button>');
  pControls.innerHTML = btns.join('');
};

window.goToPage = function(page) {
  window.currentPage = page;
  window.renderTable();
};

// REVISI: Matriks Approval Reaktif Filter Toolbar (Point, Area, BP)
window.loadApprovalMatrix = function(forceRefresh, weekIdx, monthKey) {
  var tHead = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixHead');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixBody');
  if (!tBody) return;

  var filterPt = document.getElementById('filterPoint') ? document.getElementById('filterPoint').value : '';
  var filterAr = document.getElementById('filterArea') ? document.getElementById('filterArea').value : '';
  var filterBp = document.getElementById('filterBP') ? document.getElementById('filterBP').value : '';

  if (!forceRefresh && window.rawMatrixSummaryData && window.rawMatrixSummaryData.matrix) {
    window.renderApprovalMatrixRows(window.rawMatrixSummaryData);
    return;
  }

  tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Memuat matriks data approval mingguan...</td></tr>';

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (!res || !res.matrix) {
        tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Gagal memuat matriks approval.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }
      window.rawMatrixSummaryData = res;
      window.renderApprovalMatrixFilters(res);
      window.renderApprovalMatrixRows(res);
    }).getApprovalMatrixSummary(window.currentUser, '', '', monthKey || window.currentMatrixMonthKey, weekIdx !== undefined ? weekIdx : window.currentMatrixWeekIdx, filterPt, filterAr, filterBp);
  }
};

window.renderApprovalMatrixFilters = function(res) {
  var mSelect = document.getElementById('matrixMonthSelect');
  var wSelect = document.getElementById('matrixWeekSelect');

  if (mSelect && res.months && res.months.length > 0) {
    mSelect.innerHTML = res.months.map(function(m) {
      return '<option value="' + m.key + '"' + (m.key === res.selectedMonth ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + m.label + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    window.currentMatrixMonthKey = res.selectedMonth;
  }

  if (wSelect && res.weeks && res.weeks.length > 0) {
    wSelect.innerHTML = res.weeks.map(function(w) {
      return '<option value="' + w.index + '"' + (w.index === res.currentWeekIndex ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + w.label + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    window.currentMatrixWeekIdx = res.currentWeekIndex;
  }
};

window.renderApprovalMatrixRows = function(res) {
  var tHead = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixHead');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixBody');
  if (!tHead || !tBody) return;

  var dates = res.dates || [];
  var matrix = res.matrix || [];

  tHead.innerHTML = '<tr>' +
    '<th>NIK</th>' +
    '<th>NAMA BP</th>' +
    '<th>POINT</th>' +
    '<th>AREA</th>' +
    dates.map(function(d) { return '<th style="text-align:center;">' + d + '</th>'; }).join('') +
    '<th style="color:#dc2626;text-align:center;">ZONK</th>' +
    '<th style="color:#d97706;text-align:center;">UK 1</th>' +
    '<th style="color:#15803d;text-align:center;">UK &ge; 2</th>' +
  '</tr>';

  var fPt = document.getElementById('filterPoint') ? document.getElementById('filterPoint').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  var fAr = document.getElementById('filterArea') ? document.getElementById('filterArea').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  var fBp = document.getElementById('filterBP') ? document.getElementById('filterBP').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';

  var filteredMatrix = matrix.filter(function(r) {
    if (fPt && String(r.point).toLowerCase().indexOf(fPt) === -1) return false;
    if (fAr && String(r.area).toLowerCase() !== fAr) return false;
    if (fBp && String(r.name).toLowerCase().indexOf(fBp) === -1 && String(r.nik).indexOf(fBp) === -1) return false;

    if (window.matrixStatusFilter === 'zonk') return r.zonkCount > 0;
    if (window.matrixStatusFilter === 'uk1') return r.uk1Count > 0;
    if (window.matrixStatusFilter === 'uk2') return r.uk2Count > 0;
    return true;
  });

  if (filteredMatrix.length === 0) {
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="' + (dates.length + 7) + '" class="empty">Tidak ada data BP untuk kriteria filter ini.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  tBody.innerHTML = filteredMatrix.map(function(bp) {
    var dateCells = dates.map(function(dt) {
      var st = bp.dailyStatus[dt] || 'ZONK';
      if (st === 'ZONK') return '<td style="text-align:center;"><span class="matrix-badge-zonk">ZONK</span></td>';
      if (st === 'UK 1') return '<td style="text-align:center;"><span class="matrix-badge-uk1">UK 1</span></td>';
      return '<td style="text-align:center;"><span class="matrix-badge-uk2">' + st + '</span></td>';
    }).join('');

    return '<tr>' +
      '<td>' + bp.nik + '</td>' +
      '<td><strong>' + bp.name + '</strong></td>' +
      '<td>' + bp.point + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + bp.area + '</td>' +
      dateCells +
      '<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:850;color:#dc2626;">' + bp.zonkCount + '</td>' +
      '<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:850;color:#d97706;">' + bp.uk1Count + '</td>' +
      '<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:850;color:#15803d;">' + bp.uk2Count + '</td>' +
    '</tr>';
  }).join('');
};

window.onMatrixMonthChange = function(mKey) {
  window.currentMatrixMonthKey = mKey;
  window.loadApprovalMatrix(true, 0, mKey);
};

window.onMatrixWeekChange = function(wIdx) {
  window.currentMatrixWeekIdx = parseInt(wIdx, 10) || 0;
  window.loadApprovalMatrix(true, window.currentMatrixWeekIdx, window.currentMatrixMonthKey);
};

window.filterMatrixStatus = function(status) {
  window.matrixStatusFilter = status || 'all';
  var bReset = document.getElementById('btnResetMatrixFilter');
  if (bReset) bReset.style.display = (status === 'all') ? 'none' : 'inline-block';
  if (window.rawMatrixSummaryData) {
    window.renderApprovalMatrixRows(window.rawMatrixSummaryData);
  }
};

window.toggleApprovalMatrixCollapse = function() {
  var box = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixTableContainer');
  var btn = document.getElementById('btnCollapseApprovalMatrix');
  if (!box || !btn) return;
  var isHidden = (box.style.display === 'none');
  box.style.display = isHidden ? 'block' : 'none';
  btn.textContent = isHidden ? '[-] Ciutkan' : '[+] Buka Matriks';
};

window.loadPerformaPencapaian = function(forceRefresh) {
  var topPtBody = document.getElementById('top5PointTableBody');
  var btmPtBody = document.getElementById('bottom5PointTableBody');
  var topBpBody = document.getElementById('top10BpTableBody');
  var btmBpBody = document.getElementById('bottom10BpTableBody');

  if (topPtBody) topPtBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Memuat data Top 5 Point...</td></tr>';
  if (btmPtBody) btmPtBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Memuat data Bottom 5 Point...</td></tr>';
  if (topBpBody) topBpBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Memuat data Top 10 BP...</td></tr>';
  if (btmBpBody) btmBpBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Memuat data Bottom 10 BP...</td></tr>';

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (!res || res.status !== 'success') {
        if (topPtBody) topPtBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty" style="color:#dc2626;">Gagal memuat performa.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      if (topPtBody) {
        topPtBody.innerHTML = (res.top5Point || []).map(function(pt, idx) {
          return '<tr>' +
            '<td><span class="badge-pill success">#' + (idx + 1) + '</span></td>' +
            '<td><strong>' + pt.point + '</strong></td>' +
            '<td>' + pt.area + '</td>' +
            '<td>Rp ' + (pt.totalPlafond || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
            '<td style="color:#15803d;font-weight:850;">' + (pt.achievmentTotal || 0).toFixed(1) + '%</td>' +
          '</tr>';
        }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Tidak ada data.</td></tr>';
      }

      if (btmPtBody) {
        btmPtBody.innerHTML = (res.bottom5Point || []).map(function(pt, idx) {
          return '<tr>' +
            '<td><span class="badge-pill danger">#' + (pt.ranking || (idx + 1)) + '</span></td>' +
            '<td><strong>' + pt.point + '</strong></td>' +
            '<td>' + pt.area + '</td>' +
            '<td>Rp ' + (pt.totalPlafond || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
            '<td style="color:#b91c1c;font-weight:850;">' + (pt.achievmentTotal || 0).toFixed(1) + '%</td>' +
          '</tr>';
        }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Tidak ada data.</td></tr>';
      }

      if (topBpBody) {
        topBpBody.innerHTML = (res.top10BP || []).map(function(bp, idx) {
          return '<tr>' +
            '<td><span class="badge-pill success">#' + (idx + 1) + '</span></td>' +
            '<td><strong>' + bp.name + '</strong></td>' +
            '<td>' + bp.point + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + bp.area + '</td>' +
            '<td style="color:#15803d;font-weight:850;">' + (bp.achievmentTotal || 0).toFixed(1) + '%</td>' +
          '</tr>';
        }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Tidak ada data.</td></tr>';
      }

      if (btmBpBody) {
        btmBpBody.innerHTML = (res.bottom10BP || []).map(function(bp, idx) {
          return '<tr>' +
            '<td><span class="badge-pill danger">#' + (bp.ranking || (idx + 1)) + '</span></td>' +
            '<td><strong>' + bp.name + '</strong></td>' +
            '<td>' + bp.point + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + bp.area + '</td>' +
            '<td style="color:#b91c1c;font-weight:850;">' + (bp.achievmentTotal || 0).toFixed(1) + '%</td>' +
          '</tr>';
        }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Tidak ada data.</td></tr>';
      }

    }).getPerformaPencapaianSummary(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.loadPerformaKpPerWeek = function(forceRefresh) {
  var tBody = document.getElementById('kpWeekTableBody');
  if (!tBody) return;

  if (!forceRefresh && window.kpWeekData) {
    window.renderKpWeekTable();
    return;
  }

  tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Menganalisis perbandingan mingguan & Day-to-Day KP Daily...</td></tr>';

  var fArea = document.getElementById('filterArea') ? document.getElementById('filterArea').value : '';
  var fPoint = document.getElementById('filterPoint') ? document.getElementById('filterPoint').value : '';
  var fBP = document.getElementById('filterBP') ? document.getElementById('filterBP').value : '';

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (!res || res.status !== 'success') {
        tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Gagal memuat analisis performa KP mingguan.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }
      window.kpWeekData = res;
      window.renderKpWeekTable();
    }).getPerformaKpPerWeekSummary(window.currentUser, fArea, fPoint, fBP);
  }
};

window.switchKpWeekLevel = function(level) {
  window.currentKpWeekLevel = level || 'Area';

  var tabArea = document.getElementById('tabKpWeekArea');
  var tabPoint = document.getElementById('tabKpWeekPoint');
  var tabBP = document.getElementById('tabKpWeekBP');

  [tabArea, tabPoint, tabBP].forEach(function(t) { if (t) t.classList.remove('active'); });

  if (level === 'Point' && tabPoint) tabPoint.classList.add('active');
  else if (level === 'BP' && tabBP) tabBP.classList.add('active');
  else if (tabArea) tabArea.classList.add('active');

  window.renderKpWeekTable();
};

window.renderKpWeekTable = function() {
  var tHead = document.getElementById('kpWeekTableHead');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('kpWeekTableBody');
  if (!tHead || !tBody || !window.kpWeekData) return;

  var dataset = [];
  if (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'Point') dataset = window.kpWeekData.pointSummary || [];
  else if (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'BP') dataset = window.kpWeekData.bpSummary || [];
  else dataset = window.kpWeekData.areaSummary || [];

  var selectedDay = window.currentKpWeekDay || 'all';

  var totalBulanAll = 0;
  var sumRunRate = 0;
  dataset.forEach(function(d) {
    totalBulanAll += d.totalBulan || 0;
    sumRunRate += d.weeklyRunRate || 0;
  });

  var elRunRate = document.getElementById('valWeeklyRunRate');
  var elProjEom = document.getElementById('valProjectionEOM');
  var elTotBulan = document.getElementById('valTotalBulanBerjalan');

  if (elRunRate) elRunRate.textContent = 'Rp ' + sumRunRate.toLocaleString('id-ID');
  if (elProjEom) elProjEom.textContent = 'Rp ' + (sumRunRate * 4).toLocaleString('id-ID');
  if (elTotBulan) elTotBulan.textContent = 'Rp ' + totalBulanAll.toLocaleString('id-ID');

  var entityLabel = (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'BP') ? 'NAMA BP' : (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'Point' ? 'POINT' : 'AREA');
  var daySuffix = (selectedDay !== 'all') ? (' (' + selectedDay + ')') : '';

  tHead.innerHTML = '<tr>' +
    '<th>' + entityLabel + '</th>' +
    (window.currentKpWeekLevel !== 'Area' ? '<th>AREA</th>' : '') +
    (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'BP' ? '<th>POINT</th>' : '') +
    '<th>W1' + daySuffix + '</th>' +
    '<th>W2' + daySuffix + '</th>' +
    '<th style="text-align:center;">W2 vs W1' + daySuffix + '</th>' +
    '<th>W3' + daySuffix + '</th>' +
    '<th style="text-align:center;">W3 vs W2' + daySuffix + '</th>' +
    '<th>W4' + daySuffix + '</th>' +
    '<th style="text-align:center;">W4 vs W3' + daySuffix + '</th>' +
    '<th style="color:#059669;font-weight:850;">TOTAL' + (selectedDay !== 'all' ? (' HARI ' + selectedDay.toUpperCase()) : ' BULAN') + '</th>' +
    '<th>RUN RATE MINGGUAN</th>' +
  '</tr>';

  if (dataset.length === 0) {
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Tidak ada data untuk level ' + window.currentKpWeekLevel + '.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  tBody.innerHTML = dataset.map(function(item) {
    var valW1 = 0, valW2 = 0, valW3 = 0, valW4 = 0;

    if (selectedDay === 'all') {
      valW1 = item.weeks.W1.total || 0;
      valW2 = item.weeks.W2.total || 0;
      valW3 = item.weeks.W3.total || 0;
      valW4 = item.weeks.W4.total || 0;
    } else {
      valW1 = (item.weeks.W1 && item.weeks.W1[selectedDay]) ? item.weeks.W1[selectedDay] : 0;
      valW2 = (item.weeks.W2 && item.weeks.W2[selectedDay]) ? item.weeks.W2[selectedDay] : 0;
      valW3 = (item.weeks.W3 && item.weeks.W3[selectedDay]) ? item.weeks.W3[selectedDay] : 0;
      valW4 = (item.weeks.W4 && item.weeks.W4[selectedDay]) ? item.weeks.W4[selectedDay] : 0;
    }

    var calcComp = function(curr, prev) {
      var diff = curr - prev;
      var pct = prev > 0 ? ((diff / prev) * 100).toFixed(1) : (diff > 0 ? '100.0' : '0.0');
      var isUp = diff >= 0;
      var pillClass = isUp ? 'delta-pill-up' : 'delta-pill-down';
      var sign = isUp ? '▲ +' : '▼ ';

      if (diff === 0 && curr === 0 && prev === 0) {
        return '<span class="delta-pill-same">― Rp 0</span>';
      }

      return '<span class="' + pillClass + '" title="Selisih Plafond ' + selectedDay + ': Rp ' + diff.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '">' +
        sign + 'Rp ' + Math.abs(diff).toLocaleString('id-ID') + ' (' + pct + '%)' +
      '</span>';
    };

    var comp2v1 = calcComp(valW2, valW1);
    var comp3v2 = calcComp(valW3, valW2);
    var comp4v3 = calcComp(valW4, valW3);
    var totalSelected = (valW1 + valW2 + valW3 + valW4);

    return '<tr>' +
      '<td><strong>' + item.name + '</strong></td>' +
      (window.currentKpWeekLevel !== 'Area' ? ('<td>' + (item.area || '-') + '</td>') : '') +
      (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'BP' ? ('<td>' + (item.point || '-') + '</td>') : '') +
      '<td>Rp ' + valW1.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td>Rp ' + valW2.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td style="text-align:center;">' + comp2v1 + '</td>' +
      '<td>Rp ' + valW3.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td style="text-align:center;">' + comp3v2 + '</td>' +
      '<td>Rp ' + valW4.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td style="text-align:center;">' + comp4v3 + '</td>' +
      '<td style="color:#059669;font-weight:850;">Rp ' + totalSelected.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td style="color:#0284c7;font-weight:750;">Rp ' + (item.weeklyRunRate || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
    '</tr>';
  }).join('');
};

window.renderCharts = function() {
  var key = window.currentKey;
  var data = window.filteredData;

  // 1. DASHBOARD: Donut Komposisi Aktivitas Lapangan & Line Trend 7 Hari
  if (key === 'dashboard') {
    var donutCanvas = document.getElementById('donutAktivitasCanvas');
    var trendCanvas = document.getElementById('trendCanvas');

    if (donutCanvas) {
      if (window.activeChartInstance) window.activeChartInstance.destroy();
      var dCtx = donutCanvas.getContext('2d');

      var cEligible = 0, cDO = 0, cSos = 0, cOther = 0;
      data.forEach(function(r) {
        var act = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'aktivitas') || r['_raw_6'] || r['_raw_4'] || '').toLowerCase();
        if (act.indexOf('eligible') !== -1 || act.indexOf('wl') !== -1) cEligible++;
        else if (act.indexOf('do') !== -1) cDO++;
        else if (act.indexOf('sos') !== -1 || r.is_sosialisasi) cSos++;
        else cOther++;
      });

      window.activeChartInstance = new Chart(dCtx, {
        type: 'doughnut',
        data: {
          labels: ['Kunjungan Eligible', 'Kunjungan DO', 'Sosialisasi', 'Lainnya'],
          datasets: [{
            data: [cEligible, cDO, cSos, cOther],
            backgroundColor: ['#2563eb', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#64748b'],
            borderWidth: 2,
            borderColor: '#ffffff'
          }]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: {
            legend: { position: 'right', labels: { boxWidth: 12, font: { size: 11, family: 'Inter' } } }
          },
          cutout: '62%'
        }
      });
    }

    if (trendCanvas) {
      if (window.trendChartInstance) window.trendChartInstance.destroy();
      var tCtx = trendCanvas.getContext('2d');

      var dayCounts = {};
      var dayLabels = [];
      var today = new Date();
      for (var d = 6; d >= 0; d--) {
        var targetDt = new Date(today.getTime() - (d * 86400000));
        var dtKey = ('0' + targetDt.getDate()).slice(-2) + '/' + ('0' + (targetDt.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '/' + targetDt.getFullYear();
        dayLabels.push(dtKey);
        dayCounts[dtKey] = 0;
      }

      data.forEach(function(r) {
        var dStr = window.getCellValue(r, 'tanggal') || r['_raw_16'] || r['_raw_12'] || r['_raw_0'] || '';
        var pDate = window.parseDateStr(dStr);
        if (pDate) {
          var pKey = ('0' + pDate.getDate()).slice(-2) + '/' + ('0' + (pDate.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '/' + pDate.getFullYear();
          if (dayCounts[pKey] !== undefined) dayCounts[pKey]++;
        }
      });

      var trendData = dayLabels.map(function(k) { return dayCounts[k]; });

      window.trendChartInstance = new Chart(tCtx, {
        type: 'line',
        data: {
          labels: dayLabels,
          datasets: [{
            label: 'Total Inputan Harian',
            data: trendData,
            borderColor: '#10b981',
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)',
            fill: true,
            tension: 0.35,
            pointRadius: 4,
            pointBackgroundColor: '#10b981'
          }]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
          scales: {
            y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { font: { size: 10 } } },
            x: { ticks: { font: { size: 10 } } }
          }
        }
      });
    }

  } else if (['pencapaianArea', 'pencapaianPoint', 'pencapaian'].indexOf(key) !== -1) {
    var pDonutCanvas = document.getElementById('pencapaianDonutCanvas');
    if (pDonutCanvas) {
      if (window.pencapaianDonutChartInstance) window.pencapaianDonutChartInstance.destroy();
      var pCtx = pDonutCanvas.getContext('2d');

      var sumActual = 0, sumTarget = 0;
      data.forEach(function(r) {
        sumActual += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'totaldisb') || window.getCellValue(r, 'totalplafond'));
        sumTarget += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'target') || window.getCellValue(r, 'targettotal'));
      });

      var gapTarget = Math.max(0, sumTarget - sumActual);
      var pctAch = sumTarget > 0 ? ((sumActual / sumTarget) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0.0%';

      var elTarget = document.getElementById('penCardTarget');
      var elActual = document.getElementById('penCardActual');
      var elPercent = document.getElementById('penCardPercent');
      if (elTarget) elTarget.textContent = 'Rp ' + sumTarget.toLocaleString('id-ID');
      if (elActual) elActual.textContent = 'Rp ' + sumActual.toLocaleString('id-ID');
      if (elPercent) elPercent.textContent = pctAch;

      window.pencapaianDonutChartInstance = new Chart(pCtx, {
        type: 'doughnut',
        data: {
          labels: ['Actual Plafond / Disb', 'Sisa Gap Ke Target'],
          datasets: [{
            data: [sumActual, gapTarget],
            backgroundColor: ['#10b981', '#3b82f6'],
            borderWidth: 2,
            borderColor: '#ffffff'
          }]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: {
            legend: { position: 'top', labels: { boxWidth: 12, font: { size: 11, family: 'Inter' } } }
          },
          cutout: '65%'
        }
      });
    }

  // 3. GRAFIK KOMBINASI KPI: Combo Bar & Line Chart + 7 Kartu Merah
  } else if (['kpiRM', 'kpiAM', 'kpiBM', 'kpi'].indexOf(key) !== -1) {
    var kpiCanvas = document.getElementById('kpiBarCanvas');
    if (kpiCanvas) {
      if (window.kpiBarChartInstance) window.kpiBarChartInstance.destroy();
      var kCtx = kpiCanvas.getContext('2d');

      var kTitle = document.getElementById('kpiChartTitle');
      var roleTitle = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 'RM' : (key === 'kpiAM' ? 'AM' : (key === 'kpiBM' ? 'BM' : 'BP'));
      if (kTitle) kTitle.textContent = 'Grafik Performa Skor KPI per ' + roleTitle;

      var displayRows = data.slice(0, 10);
      var kLabels = displayRows.map(function(r) { return window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || window.getCellValue(r, 'bpbm') || 'Karyawan'; });

      var dsTotalSkorLine = displayRows.map(function(r) { return window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'totalskorkpi')); });
      
      var dsScoreNew = displayRows.map(function(r) {
        var idx = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 9 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 10 : (key === 'kpiBM') ? 11 : 11;
        return window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idx] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scoremitracairnewperbulan'));
      });

      var dsScoreRenewal = displayRows.map(function(r) {
        var idx = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 13 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 14 : (key === 'kpiBM') ? 15 : 15;
        return window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idx] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerenewalmitracairperbulan'));
      });

      var dsScoreDpd0 = displayRows.map(function(r) {
        var idx = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 17 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 18 : (key === 'kpiBM') ? 19 : 19;
        return window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idx] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentratedpd0'));
      });

      var dsScoreDpd130 = displayRows.map(function(r) {
        var idx = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 21 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 22 : (key === 'kpiBM') ? 23 : 23;
        return window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idx] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentrate130daritunggakan'));
      });

      var dsScoreDpd3190 = displayRows.map(function(r) {
        var idx = (key === 'kpiRM') ? 25 : (key === 'kpiAM') ? 26 : (key === 'kpiBM') ? 27 : 27;
        return window.numVal(r['_raw_' + idx] || window.getCellValue(r, 'scorerepaymentrate3190'));
      });

      window.kpiBarChartInstance = new Chart(kCtx, {
        data: {
          labels: kLabels,
          datasets: [
            {
              type: 'line',
              label: 'Total Skor KPI (%)',
              data: dsTotalSkorLine,
              borderColor: '#1d4ed8',
              backgroundColor: '#1d4ed8',
              borderWidth: 2.5,
              pointRadius: 5,
              pointHoverRadius: 7,
              pointBackgroundColor: '#1d4ed8',
              fill: false,
              tension: 0.2,
              order: 1
            },
            {
              type: 'bar',
              label: 'Score Mitra Cair New',
              data: dsScoreNew,
              backgroundColor: '#60a5fa',
              borderRadius: 4,
              order: 2
            },
            {
              type: 'bar',
              label: 'Score % Renewal',
              data: dsScoreRenewal,
              backgroundColor: '#10b981',
              borderRadius: 4,
              order: 3
            },
            {
              type: 'bar',
              label: 'Score Repayment DPD 0',
              data: dsScoreDpd0,
              backgroundColor: '#f59e0b',
              borderRadius: 4,
              order: 4
            },
            {
              type: 'bar',
              label: 'Score Repayment DPD 1-30',
              data: dsScoreDpd130,
              backgroundColor: '#ef4444',
              borderRadius: 4,
              order: 5
            },
            {
              type: 'bar',
              label: 'Score Repayment DPD 31-90',
              data: dsScoreDpd3190,
              backgroundColor: '#8b5cf6',
              borderRadius: 4,
              order: 6
            }
          ]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          scales: {
            y: { beginAtZero: true, max: 140, ticks: { font: { size: 10 } } },
            x: { ticks: { font: { size: 10, weight: 'bold' } } }
          },
          plugins: {
            legend: { position: 'top', labels: { boxWidth: 12, font: { size: 10.5 } } }
          }
        }
      });

      // Update 7 Kartu Merah Parameter Belum Tercapai
      var cNtb = 0, cEtb = 0, c130 = 0, c3160 = 0, c6190 = 0, cDpd0 = 0, cAll = 0;
      data.forEach(function(r) {
        var param = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'parameterbelumtercapai') || '').toLowerCase();
        if (!param || param === '-') return;

        if (param.indexOf('ntb') !== -1) cNtb++;
        if (param.indexOf('etb') !== -1) cEtb++;
        if (param.indexOf('1-30') !== -1 || param.indexOf('130') !== -1) c130++;
        if (param.indexOf('31-60') !== -1) c3160++;
        if (param.indexOf('61-90') !== -1) c6190++;
        if (param.indexOf('dpd 0') !== -1 || param.indexOf('dpd0') !== -1) cDpd0++;
        if (param.indexOf('semua') !== -1 || param.indexOf('all') !== -1) cAll++;
      });

      var elNtb = document.getElementById('kpiCardNtb');
      var elEtb = document.getElementById('kpiCardEtb');
      var el130 = document.getElementById('kpiCard130');
      var el3160 = document.getElementById('kpiCard3160');
      var el6190 = document.getElementById('kpiCard6190');
      var elDpd0 = document.getElementById('kpiCardDpd0');
      var elAll = document.getElementById('kpiCardAll');

      if (elNtb) elNtb.textContent = cNtb + ' Personil';
      if (elEtb) elEtb.textContent = cEtb + ' Personil';
      if (el130) el130.textContent = c130 + ' Personil';
      if (el3160) el3160.textContent = c3160 + ' Personil';
      if (el6190) el6190.textContent = c6190 + ' Personil';
      if (elDpd0) elDpd0.textContent = cDpd0 + ' Personil';
      if (elAll) elAll.textContent = cAll + ' Personil';
    }
  }
};

window.exportChartToPNG = function() {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('donutAktivitasCanvas') || document.getElementById('pencapaianDonutCanvas') || document.getElementById('kpiBarCanvas');
  if (!canvas) return;
  var image = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  var a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = image;
  a.download = 'Grafik_Performa_' + window.currentKey + '_' + Date.now() + '.png';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
};

// REVISI: Summary Inputan Per BP Reaktif Terfilter di Semua Menu
window.renderBpSummary = function() {
  var tBody = document.getElementById('bpSummaryTableBody');
  var countEl = document.getElementById('bpSummaryCount');
  if (!tBody) return;

  var bpCounts = {};
  window.filteredData.forEach(function(r) {
    var rawBp = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'bpbm') || window.getCellValue(r, 'bp') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || r['_raw_5'] || r['_raw_1'] || '').trim();
    var pt = window.getCellValue(r, 'point') || window.getCellValue(r, 'branch') || r['_raw_4'] || r['_raw_2'] || '-';
    var ar = window.getCellValue(r, 'area') || r['_raw_3'] || '-';
    if (!rawBp || rawBp === '-') return;

    var matchedKey = null;
    for (var k in bpCounts) {
      if (window.calculateNameSimilarity(k, rawBp) >= 0.90) {
        matchedKey = k;
        break;
      }
    }

    var finalKey = matchedKey || rawBp;
    if (!bpCounts[finalKey]) {
      bpCounts[finalKey] = { name: finalKey, point: pt, area: ar, count: 0 };
    }
    bpCounts[finalKey].count++;
  });

  var list = Object.keys(bpCounts).map(function(k) { return bpCounts[k]; });
  list.sort(function(a, b) { return b.count - a.count; });

  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = list.length + ' BP Terdeteksi';

  if (list.length === 0) {
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="empty">Tidak ada data BP terdeteksi.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  tBody.innerHTML = list.map(function(b) {
    return '<tr>' +
      '<td><strong>' + b.name + '</strong></td>' +
      '<td>' + b.point + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + b.area + '</td>' +
      '<td style="font-weight:850;color:#1d4ed8;">' + b.count + ' inputan</td>' +
    '</tr>';
  }).join('');
};

// Summary Harian Inputan BP Lapangan Berwarna Sesuai Sheet
window.loadDailyBPSummary = function(forceRefresh) {
  var tBody = document.getElementById('dailyBpSummaryTableBody');
  if (!tBody) return;

  tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">Memuat ringkasan inputan harian BP lapangan...</td></tr>';

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(records) {
      if (!records || records.length === 0) {
        tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">Tidak ada aktivitas BP lapangan hari ini.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      tBody.innerHTML = records.map(function(r) {
        return '<tr>' +
          '<td><strong>' + r.bpName + '</strong></td>' +
          '<td>' + r.point + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + r.area + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + r.sosTotal + ' Visit (<span style="color:#16a34a;font-weight:800;">' + r.sosAman + ' Aman</span> / <span style="color:#dc2626;font-weight:800;">' + r.sosPalsu + ' Palsu</span>)</td>' +
          '<td>' + r.pelayananTotal + ' Majelis (<span style="color:#0284c7;font-weight:800;">' + r.pelayananD2D + ' D2D</span> • <span style="color:#16a34a;font-weight:800;">' + r.pelayananKumpul + ' Kumpul</span> • <span style="color:#d97706;font-weight:800;">' + r.pelayananSemi + ' Semi</span>)</td>' +
          '<td>' + r.wlTotal + ' Visit (<span style="color:#16a34a;font-weight:800;">' + r.wlMinat + '</span> / <span style="color:#dc2626;font-weight:800;">' + r.wlTidakMinat + '</span>)</td>' +
          '<td>' + r.doTotal + ' Visit (<span style="color:#16a34a;font-weight:800;">' + r.doMinat + '</span> / <span style="color:#dc2626;font-weight:800;">' + r.doTidakMinat + '</span>)</td>' +
          '<td>' + r.kolekTotal + ' Visit (Rp ' + (r.totalCollect || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID') + ')</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      }).join('');
    }).getDailyBPSummary(window.currentUser);
  }
};

};

window.confirmDeleteHasilInputan = function(row) {
  var subId = row.submissionId || row.submissionid || row['_raw_0'] || row['_raw_1'] || '';
  if (!subId) {
    window.showToast('ID Submission tidak valid untuk dihapus.');
    return;
  }

  window.showCustomConfirm(
    'Konfirmasi Hapus Data',
    'Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus data aktivitas (ID: ' + subId + ') ini secara permanen dari database?',
    function() {
      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.deleteHasilInputanRow === 'function') {
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            window.showLargeAlert('Berhasil', res.message, true);
            window.loadCurrentData(true);
          } else {
            window.showLargeAlert('Gagal', (res ? res.message : 'Terjadi kendala saat menghapus data'), false);
          }
        }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          window.showLargeAlert('Error', err.toString(), false);
        }).deleteHasilInputanRow({ submissionId: subId }, window.currentUser);
      }
    }
  );
};
