  body.scrollTop = body.scrollHeight;
};

window.openAuthModal = function(tab) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('authModal');
  if (!modal) return;
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
  window.switchAuthTab(tab || 'login');
};

window.closeAuthModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('authModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.switchAuthTab = function(tab) {
  var tLogin = document.getElementById('tabLoginBtn');
  var tReg = document.getElementById('tabRegisterBtn');
  var tForg = document.getElementById('tabForgotBtn');
  var fLogin = document.getElementById('loginForm');
  var fReg = document.getElementById('registerForm');
  var fForg = document.getElementById('forgotForm');
  var fSet = document.getElementById('settingsForm');
  var tabsHeader = document.getElementById('authTabsHeader');

  [tLogin, tReg, tForg].forEach(function(b) { if (b) b.classList.remove('active'); });
  [fLogin, fReg, fForg, fSet].forEach(function(f) { if (f) f.style.display = 'none'; });
  if (tabsHeader) tabsHeader.style.display = 'flex';

  if (tab === 'register') {
    if (tReg) tReg.classList.add('active');
    if (fReg) fReg.style.display = 'block';
  } else if (tab === 'forgot') {
    if (tForg) tForg.classList.add('active');
    if (fForg) fForg.style.display = 'block';
    var step1 = document.getElementById('forgotStep1');
    var step2 = document.getElementById('forgotStep2');
    if (step1) step1.style.display = 'block';
    if (step2) step2.style.display = 'none';
  } else if (tab === 'settings') {
    if (tabsHeader) tabsHeader.style.display = 'none';
    if (fSet) fSet.style.display = 'block';

    if (window.currentUser) {
      var setPhoneEl = document.getElementById('setPhone');
      var setEmailEl = document.getElementById('setEmail');
      var avatarImg = document.getElementById('settingAvatarPreview');
      var avatarInit = document.getElementById('settingAvatarInitial');

      if (setPhoneEl) setPhoneEl.value = window.currentUser.phone || '';
      if (setEmailEl) setEmailEl.value = window.currentUser.email || '';

      if (window.currentUser.photoUrl && avatarImg) {
        avatarImg.src = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(window.currentUser.photoUrl);
        avatarImg.style.display = 'block';
        if (avatarInit) avatarInit.style.display = 'none';
      } else if (avatarInit) {
        if (avatarImg) avatarImg.style.display = 'none';
        avatarInit.style.display = 'flex';
        avatarInit.textContent = (window.currentUser.name ? window.currentUser.name.charAt(0) : 'U').toUpperCase();
      }
    }
  } else {
    if (tLogin) tLogin.classList.add('active');
    if (fLogin) fLogin.style.display = 'block';
  }
};

window.handleLoginSubmit = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  var nik = document.getElementById('loginNik') ? document.getElementById('loginNik').value.trim() : '';
  var pass = document.getElementById('loginPassword') ? document.getElementById('loginPassword').value.trim() : '';
  var alertFn = typeof window.showLargeAlert === 'function' ? window.showLargeAlert : alert;
  var toastFn = typeof window.showToast === 'function' ? window.showToast : function(msg) {};

  if (!nik || !pass) return;

  var btn = event.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Memverifikasi...'; }

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.loginUser === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Masuk Akun'; }
      if (!res || res.status !== 'success') {
        alertFn('Login Gagal', (res ? res.message : 'NIK atau Password salah'), false);
        return;
      }

      window.currentUser = res.user;
      window.currentUser.loginTimestamp = Date.now();
      localStorage.setItem('zettbos_user_session', JSON.stringify(window.currentUser));

      window.closeAuthModal();
      if (typeof window.updateUserUI === 'function') window.updateUserUI();
      if (typeof window.loadCurrentData === 'function') window.loadCurrentData(true);
      toastFn('Selamat datang, ' + window.currentUser.name);
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Masuk Akun'; }
      alertFn('Error Login', err.toString(), false);
    }).loginUser(nik, pass);
  }
};

window.handleLogout = function() {
  window.currentUser = null;
  localStorage.removeItem('zettbos_user_session');
  if (typeof window.updateUserUI === 'function') window.updateUserUI();
  if (typeof window.openMenu === 'function') window.openMenu('dashboard');
  if (typeof window.showToast === 'function') window.showToast('Anda telah logout dari aplikasi');
};

window.handleRegisterSubmit = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  var nik = document.getElementById('regNik') ? document.getElementById('regNik').value.trim() : '';
  var phone = document.getElementById('regPhone') ? document.getElementById('regPhone').value.trim() : '';
  var email = document.getElementById('regEmail') ? document.getElementById('regEmail').value.trim() : '';
  var role = document.getElementById('regRole') ? document.getElementById('regRole').value : '';
  var pass = document.getElementById('regPassword') ? document.getElementById('regPassword').value.trim() : '';
  var alertFn = typeof window.showLargeAlert === 'function' ? window.showLargeAlert : alert;

  if (!nik || !phone || !email || !role || !pass) {
    alertFn('Data Tidak Lengkap', 'Semua kolom registrasi wajib diisi.', false);
    return;
  }

  var btn = document.getElementById('btnSubmitRegister');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Memverifikasi Data MPP...'; }

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.registerUser === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Daftarkan Akun & Verifikasi MPP'; }
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        alertFn('Registrasi Berhasil', res.message, true);
        window.switchAuthTab('login');
      } else {
        alertFn('Registrasi Ditolak', (res ? res.message : 'Terjadi kendala saat verifikasi'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Daftarkan Akun & Verifikasi MPP'; }
      alertFn('Error Sistem', err.toString(), false);
    }).registerUser({ nik: nik, phone: phone, email: email, role: role, password: pass });
  }
};

window.handleForgotSubmit = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  var nik = document.getElementById('forgotNik') ? document.getElementById('forgotNik').value.trim() : '';
  var contact = document.getElementById('forgotContact') ? document.getElementById('forgotContact').value.trim() : '';
  var alertFn = typeof window.showLargeAlert === 'function' ? window.showLargeAlert : alert;

  if (!nik || !contact) return;

  var btn = document.getElementById('btnSendForgotOtp');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Mengirim Kode OTP...'; }

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.forgotPassword === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Kirim Kode OTP Pemulihan'; }
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        var step1 = document.getElementById('forgotStep1');
        var step2 = document.getElementById('forgotStep2');
        var maskedEl = document.getElementById('forgotEmailMasked');
        if (step1) step1.style.display = 'none';
        if (step2) step2.style.display = 'block';
        if (maskedEl) maskedEl.textContent = res.emailMasked || '-';
        window.showToast('Kode OTP telah dikirim ke email terdaftar Anda');
      } else {
        alertFn('Gagal Memproses', (res ? res.message : 'Data NIK dan Email tidak cocok'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Kirim Kode OTP Pemulihan'; }
      alertFn('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).forgotPassword(nik, contact);
  }
};

window.handleVerifyResetOtp = function() {
  var nik = document.getElementById('forgotNik') ? document.getElementById('forgotNik').value.trim() : '';
  var otp = document.getElementById('forgotOtpCode') ? document.getElementById('forgotOtpCode').value.trim() : '';
  var newPass = document.getElementById('forgotNewPassword') ? document.getElementById('forgotNewPassword').value.trim() : '';
  var alertFn = typeof window.showLargeAlert === 'function' ? window.showLargeAlert : alert;

  if (!nik || !otp || !newPass) {
    alertFn('Data Tidak Lengkap', 'Silakan masukkan 6-digit kode OTP dan kata sandi baru Anda!', false);
    return;
  }

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.verifyOtpAndResetPassword === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        alertFn('Password Berhasil Direset', res.message, true);
        var step1 = document.getElementById('forgotStep1');
        var step2 = document.getElementById('forgotStep2');
        if (step1) step1.style.display = 'block';
        if (step2) step2.style.display = 'none';
        window.switchAuthTab('login');
      } else {
        alertFn('Verifikasi Gagal', (res ? res.message : 'Kode OTP tidak valid atau kedaluwarsa!'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      alertFn('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).verifyOtpAndResetPassword({ nik: nik, otp: otp, newPassword: newPass });
  }
};

window.handleRequestProfileOtp = function() {
  var toastFn = typeof window.showToast === 'function' ? window.showToast : function(msg) {};
  var alertFn = typeof window.showLargeAlert === 'function' ? window.showLargeAlert : alert;
  var otpSection = document.getElementById('otpSection');
  var btnOtp = document.getElementById('btnRequestOtp');
  var btnSave = document.getElementById('btnVerifySave');

  if (btnOtp) { btnOtp.disabled = true; btnOtp.textContent = 'Mengirim OTP ke Email...'; }

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.requestOtpForProfileUpdate === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (btnOtp) { btnOtp.disabled = false; btnOtp.textContent = 'Kirim Kode OTP Verifikasi Email'; }
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        if (otpSection) otpSection.style.display = 'block';
        if (btnOtp) btnOtp.style.display = 'none';
        if (btnSave) btnSave.style.display = 'block';
        toastFn('Kode OTP verifikasi terkirim ke email ' + (res.emailMasked || 'terdaftar'));
      } else {
        alertFn('Gagal Kirim OTP', (res ? res.message : 'Tidak dapat mengirim kode OTP'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      if (btnOtp) { btnOtp.disabled = false; btnOtp.textContent = 'Kirim Kode OTP Verifikasi Email'; }
      alertFn('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).requestOtpForProfileUpdate(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.handleSettingsSubmit = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  var phone = document.getElementById('setPhone') ? document.getElementById('setPhone').value.trim() : '';
  var email = document.getElementById('setEmail') ? document.getElementById('setEmail').value.trim() : '';
  var oldPass = document.getElementById('setOldPassword') ? document.getElementById('setOldPassword').value.trim() : '';
  var newPass = document.getElementById('setNewPassword') ? document.getElementById('setNewPassword').value.trim() : '';
  var otp = document.getElementById('setOtpInput') ? document.getElementById('setOtpInput').value.trim() : '';
  var alertFn = typeof window.showLargeAlert === 'function' ? window.showLargeAlert : alert;

  if (!oldPass) {
    alertFn('Verifikasi Diperlukan', 'Masukkan password saat ini untuk menyimpan perubahan profil.', false);
    return;
  }

  var btn = document.getElementById('btnVerifySave');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan...'; }

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.updateUserProfile === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Verifikasi OTP & Simpan Profil'; }
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        alertFn('Profil Diperbarui', res.message, true);
        if (window.currentUser) {
          window.currentUser.phone = phone;
          window.currentUser.email = email;
          localStorage.setItem('zettbos_user_session', JSON.stringify(window.currentUser));
        }
        window.closeAuthModal();
      } else {
        alertFn('Gagal Memperbarui', (res ? res.message : 'Password salah / OTP tidak valid'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Verifikasi OTP & Simpan Profil'; }
      alertFn('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).updateUserProfile({ phone: phone, email: email, oldPassword: oldPass, newPassword: newPass, otp: otp }, window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.handleProfilePhotoSelect = function(event) {
  var file = event.target.files[0];
  if (!file || !window.currentUser) return;

  var toastFn = typeof window.showToast === 'function' ? window.showToast : function(msg) {};
  var reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) {
    var img = new Image();
    img.onload = function() {
      var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width = 200;
      canvas.height = 200;
      var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, 200, 200);

      var base64 = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.uploadFotoProfil === 'function') {
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            window.currentUser.photoUrl = res.photoUrl;
            localStorage.setItem('zettbos_user_session', JSON.stringify(window.currentUser));
            if (typeof window.updateUserUI === 'function') window.updateUserUI();
            toastFn('Foto profil CDN Google berhasil diperbarui!');
          }
        }).uploadFotoProfil({ photoBase64: base64 }, window.currentUser);
      }
    };
    img.src = e.target.result;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
};

window.openUserProfileModal = function(user) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('userProfileModal');
  if (!modal || !user) return;

  var elName = document.getElementById('popUserName');
  var elRole = document.getElementById('popUserRole');
  var elNik = document.getElementById('popUserNik');
  var elGender = document.getElementById('popUserGender');
  var elMasa = document.getElementById('popUserMasaKerja');
  var elStatus = document.getElementById('popUserStatusKaryawan');
  var elPosisi = document.getElementById('popUserPosisi');
  var elPt = document.getElementById('popUserPoint');
  var elAr = document.getElementById('popUserArea');
  var elReg = document.getElementById('popUserRegional');
  var elAvatar = document.getElementById('popUserAvatar');
  var elInit = document.getElementById('popUserInitial');
  var cleanFn = typeof window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl === 'function' ? window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl : function(u) { return u || ''; };

  if (elName) elName.textContent = user.name || '-';
  if (elRole) elRole.textContent = user.role || '-';
  if (elNik) elNik.textContent = user.nik || '-';
  if (elGender) elGender.textContent = user.gender || '-';
  if (elMasa) elMasa.textContent = user.masaKerja || '-';
  if (elStatus) elStatus.textContent = user.statusKaryawan || '-';
  if (elPosisi) elPosisi.textContent = user.posisi || user.role || '-';
  if (elPt) elPt.textContent = user.point || '-';
  if (elAr) elAr.textContent = user.area || '-';
  if (elReg) elReg.textContent = user.regional || 'Gorontalo';

  var photoSrc = user.photoUrl ? cleanFn(user.photoUrl) : '';
  if (photoSrc && elAvatar) {
    elAvatar.src = photoSrc;
    elAvatar.style.display = 'block';
    if (elInit) elInit.style.display = 'none';
  } else if (elInit) {
    if (elAvatar) elAvatar.style.display = 'none';
    elInit.style.display = 'flex';
    elInit.textContent = (user.name ? user.name.charAt(0) : 'U').toUpperCase();
  }

  if ((!user.gender || user.gender === '-' || !user.masaKerja || user.masaKerja === '-') && typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getUserMppProfile === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (res && res.status === 'success' && res.profile) {
        if (elGender) elGender.textContent = res.profile.gender || '-';
        if (elMasa) elMasa.textContent = res.profile.masaKerja || '-';
        if (elStatus) elStatus.textContent = res.profile.statusKaryawan || '-';
        if (elPosisi) elPosisi.textContent = res.profile.posisi || user.role || '-';
        if (elPt && (!user.point || user.point === '-')) elPt.textContent = res.profile.point || '-';
        if (elAr && (!user.area || user.area === '-')) elAr.textContent = res.profile.area || '-';
      }
    }).getUserMppProfile(user.nik);
  }

  window.selectedDmUser = user;
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closeUserProfileModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('userProfileModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.directMessageToUser = function() {
  if (!window.selectedDmUser) return;
  window.closeUserProfileModal();
  window.toggleChatBox();

  var selScope = document.getElementById('chatScopeSelect');
  var selTarget = document.getElementById('chatTargetBPSelect');
  if (selScope && selTarget) {
    selScope.value = 'direct';
    window.handleScopeChange();
    selTarget.value = window.selectedDmUser.nik;
  }
};

window.toggleTopOnlinePopup = function() {
  var popup = document.getElementById('topOnlinePopup');
  if (!popup) return;
  var isHidden = (popup.style.display === 'none' || popup.style.display === '');
  popup.style.display = isHidden ? 'block' : 'none';
};

window.toggleDuplicateFilter = function() {
  window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly = !window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly;
  var btn = document.getElementById('btnFilterDuplicate');
  if (btn) {
    btn.textContent = window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly ? 'Tampilkan Semua Data' : 'Lihat Data Duplikat';
    btn.style.background = window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly ? '#15803d' : '#ea580c';
  }

  if (window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly) {
    var counts = {};
    window.allData.forEach(function(r) {
      var rawId = window.getCellValue(r, 'idloan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'loan_id') || '';
      var cleanId = window.normalizeNumericId(rawId);
      var rawName = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'namamitra') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var key = cleanId ? ('ID_' + cleanId) : (rawName && rawName !== '-' ? ('NM_' + rawName) : '');

      if (key) {
        counts[key] = (counts[key] || 0) + 1;
      }
    });

    window.filteredData = window.allData.filter(function(r) {
      var rawId2 = window.getCellValue(r, 'idloan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'loan_id') || '';
      var cleanId2 = window.normalizeNumericId(rawId2);
      var rawName2 = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'namamitra') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var key2 = cleanId2 ? ('ID_' + cleanId2) : (rawName2 && rawName2 !== '-' ? ('NM_' + rawName2) : '');

      return (key2 && counts[key2] > 1);
    });
  } else {
    if (typeof window.applyFilter === 'function') window.applyFilter();
    return;
  }

  window.currentPage = 1;
  if (typeof window.renderTable === 'function') window.renderTable();
};

window.openVisitDetailModal = function(row) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('visitDetailModal');
  if (!modal) return;

  var getCellFn = typeof window.getCellValue === 'function' ? window.getCellValue : function(r, k) { return r[k] || '-'; };
  var cleanFn = typeof window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl === 'function' ? window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl : function(u) { return u || ''; };
  var actFn = typeof window.getDefaultActivityName === 'function' ? window.getDefaultActivityName : function() { return 'Aktivitas'; };

  var elDate = document.getElementById('vdTanggalWaktu');
  var elPetugas = document.getElementById('vdPetugas');
  var elAreaPoint = document.getElementById('vdAreaPoint');
  var elAct = document.getElementById('vdAktivitas');
  var elMajelis = document.getElementById('vdMajelis');
  var elLoanMitra = document.getElementById('vdLoanMitra');
  var elKet = document.getElementById('vdKeterangan');
  var btnPhoto = document.getElementById('vdBtnPhoto');

  var dVal = (getCellFn(row, 'tanggal') || getCellFn(row, 'tgl') || '-') + ' ' + (getCellFn(row, 'waktu') || '');
  var bpVal = getCellFn(row, 'bpbm') || getCellFn(row, 'bp') || getCellFn(row, 'nama') || '-';
  var ptVal = getCellFn(row, 'point') || getCellFn(row, 'branch') || '-';
  var arVal = getCellFn(row, 'area') || '-';
  var actVal = getCellFn(row, 'aktivitas') || actFn(window.currentKey);
  var majVal = getCellFn(row, 'majelis') || '-';
  var loanVal = getCellFn(row, 'idloan') || getCellFn(row, 'loan_id') || '-';
  var mitraVal = getCellFn(row, 'namamitra') || getCellFn(row, 'borrower') || getCellFn(row, 'customer_name') || '-';
  var hstVal = getCellFn(row, 'hasilvisit') || '';
  var ketVal = getCellFn(row, 'keterangan') || '';
  var fullText = (hstVal && ketVal && hstVal !== ketVal) ? (hstVal + '\n\nCatatan Tambahan:\n' + ketVal) : (hstVal || ketVal || '-');

  if (elDate) elDate.textContent = dVal.trim();
  if (elPetugas) elPetugas.textContent = bpVal;
  if (elAreaPoint) elAreaPoint.textContent = arVal + ' • ' + ptVal;
  if (elAct) elAct.textContent = actVal;
  if (elMajelis) elMajelis.textContent = majVal;
  if (elLoanMitra) elLoanMitra.textContent = (loanVal !== '-' ? (loanVal + ' • ') : '- • ') + (mitraVal !== '-' ? mitraVal : '-');
  if (elKet) elKet.textContent = fullText;

  var photoUrl = cleanFn(getCellFn(row, 'dokumentasifoto') || getCellFn(row, 'foto') || getCellFn(row, 'bukti_url') || getCellFn(row, 'bukti_foto_kunjungan') || row['_raw_8'] || row['_raw_7'] || '');
  window.activeVisitDetailPhotoUrl = photoUrl;

  if (btnPhoto) {
    btnPhoto.style.display = photoUrl ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
  }

  modal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closeVisitDetailModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('visitDetailModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
  window.activeVisitDetailPhotoUrl = '';
};

window.openVisitBuktiFoto = function() {
  if (!window.activeVisitDetailPhotoUrl) return;
  window.openPhotoViewerModal(window.activeVisitDetailPhotoUrl, 'Bukti Dokumentasi Visit');
};

window.initApp = function() {
  var savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('zettbos_theme') || 'light';
  if (typeof window.setThemeMode === 'function') window.setThemeMode(savedTheme);

  var savedLang = localStorage.getItem('zettbos_lang') || 'id';
  if (typeof window.setLanguage === 'function') window.setLanguage(savedLang);

  var savedSession = localStorage.getItem('zettbos_user_session');
  if (savedSession) {
    try {
      var sessObj = JSON.parse(savedSession);
      var now = Date.now();
      var maxDur = window.SESSION_MAX_DURATION || (3 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      if (sessObj && sessObj.loginTimestamp && (now - sessObj.loginTimestamp < maxDur)) {
        window.currentUser = sessObj;
      } else {
        localStorage.removeItem('zettbos_user_session');
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  if (typeof window.updateUserUI === 'function') window.updateUserUI();

  if (typeof window.initDraggableAssistant === 'function') {
    window.initDraggableAssistant();
  }

  if (navigator.geolocation) {
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
      function(pos) {
        window.currentLat = pos.coords.latitude;
        window.currentLng = pos.coords.longitude;
        if (window.currentUser && typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.updateHeartbeat === 'function') {
          google.script.run.updateHeartbeat(window.currentUser, window.currentLat, window.currentLng);
        }
      },
      function(err) {
        console.warn('Izin lokasi GPS belum diaktifkan:', err.message);
      },
      { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 15000, maximumAge: 0 }
    );
  }

  if (window.currentUser && ['1-RM', '2-AM', '3-BM', 'Editor', 'Admin'].indexOf(window.currentUser.role) !== -1) {
    window.loadTrackingData();
  }

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getBPList === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(bps) {
      window.cachedBpList = bps || [];
    }).getBPList();
  }

  if (typeof window.openMenu === 'function') {
    window.openMenu('dashboard');
  }

  setInterval(function() {
    if (window.currentUser && typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.updateHeartbeat === 'function') {
      google.script.run.updateHeartbeat(window.currentUser, window.currentLat, window.currentLng);
    }
  }, 60000);

  setInterval(function() {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getOnlineUsers === 'function') {
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(users) {
        window.onlineUsersList = users || [];
        var elCount = document.getElementById('topOnlineCount');
        if (elCount) elCount.textContent = window.onlineUsersList.length;

        var popup = document.getElementById('topOnlinePopup');
        var cleanFn = typeof window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl === 'function' ? window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl : function(u) { return u || ''; };

        if (popup) {
          popup.innerHTML = window.onlineUsersList.map(function(u) {
            var pSrc = u.photoUrl ? cleanFn(u.photoUrl) : '';
            var avHtml = pSrc ?
              ('<img class="online-user-avatar" src="' + pSrc + '" alt="" />') :
              ('<div class="online-user-avatar-initial">' + (u.name ? u.name.charAt(0) : 'U') + '</div>');

            return '<div class="online-user-item" onclick="window.openUserProfileModal(' + JSON.stringify(u).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + ')">' +
              avHtml +
              '<div class="online-user-text-col">' +
                '<span class="online-user-name">' + u.name + '</span>' +
                '<span class="online-user-sub">' + u.role + ' • ' + u.point + '</span>' +
              '</div>' +
            '</div>';
          }).join('');
        }
      }).getOnlineUsers();
    }
  }, 25000);

  setInterval(function() {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getGlobalChatMessages === 'function') {
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(msgs) {
        if (!msgs || msgs.length === 0) return;
        var myNik = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.nik).trim() : '';
        var unread = 0;

        msgs.forEach(function(m) {
          if (m.senderNik !== myNik && !m.isRead) unread++;
        });

        window.unreadChatCount = unread;
        var badge = document.getElementById('chatUnreadBadge');
        if (badge) {
          if (unread > 0) {
            badge.textContent = unread;
            badge.style.display = 'flex';
          } else {
            badge.style.display = 'none';
          }
        }

        var box = document.getElementById('chatBoxModal');
        if (box && box.style.display === 'flex') {
          window.renderChatMessages(msgs, false);
        }
      }).getGlobalChatMessages(window.currentUser);
    }
  }, 10000);
};

window.toggleSidebar = function() {
  var sb = document.getElementById('sidebar');
  var ov = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
  if (!sb || !ov) return;
  sb.classList.toggle('show');
  ov.classList.toggle('show');
};

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  if (typeof window.setThemeMode === 'function') {
    window.initApp();
  }
});
</script>
